We characterize the resulting trade-off: positive within-group coupling can only inflate the variance of realized expert loads relative to independent routing, while nonnegative cross-group opposition can only reduce it relative to flat coupling at the same within-group strength. Coherence and load dispersion are thus controlled by two complementary dependence dials on the invariance constraint surface. Because the base model is untouched, the dials can be driven by a small controller over frozen features, trainable with a score-function estimator: the frozen network is evaluated only in the forward direction, and gradients are confined to the controller. An initial small-scale pilot validates the mechanism and the training route, but does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains.

</details>


### [132] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出名为LAWFUL的框架，旨在解决神经网络在物理定律学习中的可解释性差距，包括覆盖感知的因果一致性度量、电路有效域测试、定律不变性和禁止行为验证，以及物理量在电路中流动的量化。通过在Mocap2Radar变压器上验证多普勒频率定律的学习与内部使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经网络虽能准确预测物理系统，但难以确认其是否真正学习了形式化的结构化知识（如物理定律），以及网络内部计算是否在整个定律有效域内使用该表示。现有方法在连续变量物理定律的可解释性上存在四个空白：缺乏覆盖连续反事实的因果一致性度量、缺乏已识别电路的有效域测试、缺乏定律不变性和禁止行为的验证、缺乏推导物理量在电路中流动的量化。

Method: 开发LAWFUL框架，通过覆盖感知的因果一致性度量（覆盖连续反事实）和电路有效域测试来填补前两个空白，并为后两个空白奠定基础。在Mocap2Radar变压器上验证该框架，检查是否学习并内部使用多普勒频率定律f(t)=2v(t)/λ，其中f(t)和v(t)均未直接出现在数据中。

Result: LAWFUL框架能够评估神经网络是否学习并内部使用物理定律，通过覆盖感知的因果一致性度量和有效域测试，在Mocap2Radar案例中验证了多普勒定律的表示与使用。

Conclusion: LAWFUL为验证神经网络是否真正学习物理定律并理解其内部计算提供了系统方法，初步解决了连续变量物理定律可解释性的关键空白，未来可扩展至后两个空白（定律不变性、禁止行为验证，以及物理量流动量化）。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [133] [MPP-GNN: Subject-Adaptive Community Detection for fMRI-Based Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28681)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种元概率池化图神经网络（MPP-GNN），通过自适应图分区发现个体特定的脑功能模块，并以此为先验指导边细化和表示学习，在阿尔茨海默病分类任务上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图神经网络的脑功能连接分析方法通常假设所有被试具有预设数量的功能模块，忽略了被试间差异性；且发现的模块很少被直接用于指导学习到的连接模式。

Method: 将模型任务构建为耦合的双层优化，通过分层自适应图分区发现被试特定的脑模块，然后将发现的脑模块作为显式先验指导边细化和表示学习。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上对阿尔茨海默病分类取得了最高的AUC；与Yeo脑图谱定义的典型功能网络组织显著对齐，并揭示了AD的网络级去分化模式。

Conclusion: MPP-GNN能够有效捕捉被试间脑功能连接的变异性，利用个体化的功能模块指导连接模式学习，提升了AD分类性能并发现了有意义的神经生物学模式。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a widely used technique for studying the brain. Recent methods that utilize graph neural networks (GNNs) for analysis of brain functional connectivity have shown great potential for the classification of brain disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, these methods often assume a preset number of functional modules across all subjects, which overlooks inter-subject variability. In addition, the discovered modules are rarely used to directly guide the learned connectivity patterns. Here, to address these issues, we propose a Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN (MPP-GNN). We frame the model's task as a coupled, bilevel optimization that performs adaptive graph partitioning hierarchically to discover subject-specific modules and then uses the discovered brain modules as an explicit prior to guide edge refinement and representation learning. We validate MPP-GNN on two public datasets for AD classification, achieving the highest AUC in comparison to established baselines for both datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that MPP-GNN shows significant alignment with the canonical functional-network organization defined by the Yeo brain atlas and reveals a network-level dedifferentiation pattern for AD.

</details>


### [134] [Technological Advances in Detecting and Managing Cognitive Impairment in Older Adults: Trends, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28687)
*Mohammad Asif,Azizuddin Khan,Mohd Azam,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了用于老年人认知障碍检测和管理的最新科技进展，涵盖神经电生理信号（EEG）、结构/分子神经影像（MRI、淀粉样蛋白/tau PET）、血液生物标志物和数字标记，并整合人工智能、机器学习和深度学习技术。文章提出了跨学科分类法、方法论严谨性视角（强调受试者独立和场地独立验证）、整合早期检测框架以及比较表格。EEG标记和深度模型报告了较高准确率，但很多基于小规模单中心数据集；血浆p-tau217已具备临床实用性，首个血液检测将于2025年获批辅助阿尔茨海默病诊断；抗淀粉样蛋白疗法已获批但收益有限；多领域生活方式预防趋于成熟。可穿戴、远程、语音和虚拟现实工具实现了连续生态化监测，多模态融合提升了灵敏度和特异性。当前障碍包括标准化、可解释性、数据隐私和公平部署。未来方向在于可靠、多模态、纵向验证的系统，将早期检测与个性化干预衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人口老龄化，从轻度认知障碍（MCI）到痴呆的认知衰退是未来几十年关键的健康挑战，但常规评估常常遗漏其最早期的迹象。现有技术分散在不同领域，缺乏系统性整合和跨学科视角，且许多方法缺乏严格的外部验证。因此需要一篇批判性综述，综合神经生理、神经影像、血液标志物和数字标志物等多模态技术进展，并建立统一的早期检测框架。

Method: 本文采用批判性综合方法，对神经电生理信号（EEG）、结构/分子神经影像（MRI、淀粉样蛋白/tau PET）、血液生物标志物（如p-tau217）和数字标志物（可穿戴、远程、语音、虚拟现实工具）等多模态技术进行系统梳理，整合AI/ML/DL方法（CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、Transformer、自监督EEG基础模型）。文章贡献了跨学科分类法、强调受试者和场地独立验证的方法论严谨性视角、分层筛查到干预衔接的整合早期检测框架，以及检测方法、干预措施和风险/保护因素的对比表格。

Result: EEG标记（α/θ变化、P300潜伏期）和深度模型报告了较高准确率，但多数基于小规模单中心数据集，难以通过严格的外部验证；血浆p-tau217已达到临床实用性水平，首个血液检测将于2025年获批辅助阿尔茨海默病诊断；抗淀粉样蛋白疗法（lecanemab、donanemab）已获批但收益有限且有争议；多领域生活方式预防策略已趋于成熟；可穿戴、远程、语音和虚拟现实工具实现了连续和生态效度高的监测；多模态融合提升了灵敏度和特异性。障碍包括标准化不足、可解释性差、数据隐私问题和公平部署挑战。

Conclusion: 该领域的近期前景在于构建可靠、多模态、纵向验证的系统，将早期检测与可操作的个性化护理无缝衔接。未来需要克服标准化、可解释性、数据隐私和公平部署等障碍，确保技术在真实世界中的稳健外推和公平应用。

Abstract: As populations age, cognitive decline from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia is a defining health challenge of the coming decades, yet routine assessment often misses its earliest signs. This article critically synthesizes recent technological advances for detecting and managing cognitive impairment in older adults, spanning neurophysiological signals (chiefly electroencephalography, EEG), structural and molecular neuroimaging (MRI and amyloid/tau PET), blood-based biomarkers, and digital markers, integrated through artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning (ML), and deep learning (DL). Beyond summarizing, it contributes a cross-disciplinary taxonomy, a methodological-rigor lens foregrounding subject- and site-independent validation, an integrative early-detection framework linking tiered screening to intervention, and comparison tables of detection methods, interventions, and risk and protective factors. EEG markers (alpha/theta changes, P300 latency) and deep models (CNNs, LSTM/BiLSTM, transformers, self-supervised EEG foundation models) report strong accuracy, yet many rest on small, single-site datasets unlikely to survive rigorous external validation. Elsewhere, gains are tangible: plasma p-tau217 has reached clinical utility, with the first blood test cleared to aid Alzheimer's diagnosis in 2025; anti-amyloid therapies (lecanemab, donanemab) are approved despite modest, contested benefits; and multidomain lifestyle prevention has matured. Wearable, remote, speech, and virtual-reality tools enable continuous, ecologically valid monitoring, and multimodal fusion improves sensitivity and specificity. Barriers remain: standardization, explainability, data privacy, and equitable, externally validated deployment. The field's near-term promise lies in trustworthy, multimodal, longitudinally validated systems linking early detection to actionable, personalized care.

</details>


### [135] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对工业非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）中噪声和并发运行导致泛化困难的问题，提出了一种轻量级Seq2Point扩展模型SEDR-Seq2P，通过膨胀残差块和压缩-激励注意力机制，在IMDELD数据集上以更低的推理延迟实现了比基线更优的精度-延迟平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业NILM面临测量噪声和广泛的同时机器运行挑战，使得基于住宅数据调优的模型泛化能力不足。现有方法在精度与计算延迟之间难以取得理想平衡，亟需一种适用于工业场景的可扩展轻量级方案。

Method: 采用一对多、多任务分解设置，使单一网络从总功率中估计多个工业机器负载。在统一评估协议下对Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU和WaveNet进行基准测试，并提出SEDR-Seq2P——一种在Seq2Point基础上引入膨胀残差块和压缩-激励注意力的轻量级扩展。

Result: 相比Seq2Point基线，SEDR-Seq2P的MAE降低约7%，决定系数提升约1%，匹配率提高约0.8%。相比WaveNet，推理延迟降低约58%，实现了更优的精度-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: SEDR-Seq2P在工业NILM中展现出良好的精度-延迟平衡，适合大规模工业部署，为工业负荷监测提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%. In addition, compared to WaveNet, SEDR-Seq2P reduces inference latency by approximately 58%, yielding a favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment.

</details>


### [136] [Predicting Steel Fatigue Life from Micrographs Using Physics-Informed Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28695)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种计算机视觉框架(CV)，无需物理测试，直接从光学显微图像中估算轻质合金钢的疲劳寿命(log Nf)，并开源了流水线和合成数据集生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疲劳寿命评估需要数十至数百小时的力学测试，难以用于快速质量控制，因此需要更高效的无损评估方法。

Method: 该流程包含七阶段OpenCV预处理、28维物理启示特征提取（量化裂纹形态、晶粒结构、孔隙率和织构），以及采用高斯负对数似然损失的CNN回归模型，同时预测log Nf和样本特异性不确定性。评估了SE-CNN、ResNet-50、VGG-16三种架构。

Result: 在合成显微图像基准上，ResNet-50取得R²=0.93、RMSE=0.18对数周期、macro-F1=0.91；GNLL损失使期望校准误差降低76%（ECE: 0.089→0.021）；Grad-CAM表明网络关注冶金学意义的微观特征；单张图像处理时间低于65 ms。

Conclusion: 虽然验证完全基于合成显微图像，但结果证明了该方法在模拟条件下的方法论合理性；下一步计划对真实现场样本进行领域迁移研究。

Abstract: Here is the plain text version optimized for arXiv's submission form. Custom macros (like \CV and \SI) have been converted to standard text/math so they render correctly on the webpage: Evaluating the fatigue life of structural steels conventionally requires mechanical testing lasting tens to hundreds of hours, making it impractical for rapid quality control. We present CV, a computer vision framework that estimates the fatigue life ($\log N_f$) of lightweight alloy steels directly from optical micrographs without physical testing.The pipeline features a seven-stage OpenCV preprocessing routine to remove artifacts, a 28-dimensional physics-informed feature extractor (quantifying crack morphology, grain structure, porosity, and texture), and a CNN regression model trained with a Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) loss to jointly predict $\log N_f$ and sample-specific uncertainty $\hatσ$.Evaluating three architectures (SE-CNN, ResNet-50, VGG-16) on a synthetic micrograph benchmark, ResNet-50 achieves $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE = 0.18 log-cycles, and macro-F1 = 0.91. The GNLL objective reduces Expected Calibration Error by 76% compared to a mean-squared-error baseline (ECE: $0.089 \rightarrow 0.021$). Grad-CAM maps confirm the network attends to metallurgically meaningful microstructural features.Running in under 65 ms per image, the pipeline and synthetic dataset generator are open-sourced. Because validation relies entirely on synthetic micrographs, these results demonstrate methodological soundness under simulated conditions; a domain-transfer study on real field samples is the immediate next step.

</details>


### [137] [Mitigating Class-Tail Undercoverage in Medical Vision-Language Models under Clinical Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28696)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CALCoDe，一种用于冷冻医学视觉语言模型（VLM）的后处理可靠性层，旨在解决现有共形预测方法在临床分布偏移下对个别疾病类别的覆盖不足问题。CALCoDe 通过交叉拟合验证识别高风险类别，结合类别尾部阈值与局部共形阈值，并引入支持审计来延迟低支持度的样本。在 HAM10000 到 ISIC 2019 和 HAM10000 到 PAD-UFES-20 两个皮肤科偏移数据集以及四种 VLM 骨干网络上，CALCoDe 是所有方法中唯一在所有八种设置下同时达到 0.95 边际和最差类别接受覆盖的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在临床分布偏移后虽然能保持较高的观测边际覆盖，但会严重低估某个疾病类别的覆盖。受影响的类别随采集协议和骨干网络几何结构变化，因此源分布患病率无法可靠提示失败。现有的局部共形方法和尾部感知共形方法分别适应测试邻域和源频率尾部，但未能建模保留类别的类别级覆盖不足问题。

Method: CALCoDe 包含三个步骤：1) 交叉拟合验证预测识别有覆盖不足风险的类别；2) 在独立的校准集上估计这些类别的条件尾部阈值；3) 将每个受保护阈值与局部共形阈值通过单侧最大值结合，形成最终预测集。此外，通过独立校准的支持审计，将缺乏足够内部支持的样本延迟。在受保护类别内可交换性假设下，CALCoDe 提供有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在两个皮肤科分布偏移（HAM10000 到 ISIC 2019 和 HAM10000 到 PAD-UFES-20）和四个冻结 VLM 骨干网络（BiomedCLIP、OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32、PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32、MedSigLIP-448）上，CALCoDe 是唯一在所有八种设置中边际和最差类别接受覆盖均达到 0.95 的方法。在 HAM10000 到 ISIC 2019 上，其平均最差类别接受覆盖为 0.970，而 sTACP 为 0.926，LCP-VLM 为 0.864。

Conclusion: CALCoDe 能有效解决医学 VLM 在分布偏移下的类级覆盖不足问题，提供可靠的预测集且保持预设的覆盖保证。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can retain high observed marginal coverage after clinical shift while substantially under-covering an individual disease class. The affected class varies with acquisition protocol and backbone geometry, so source prevalence does not reliably reveal the failure. Existing localized and tail-aware conformal methods respectively adapt to test neighborhoods and source-frequency tails, leaving held-out class-wise coverage failure unmodeled. We introduce Class-Tail Adaptive Localized Conformal Deferral (CALCoDe), a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical VLMs. Cross-fitted validation predictions identify classes at risk of undercoverage, and a disjoint calibration split estimates their class-conditional tail thresholds. CALCoDe combines each protected threshold with a localized conformal threshold using a one-sided maximum. The resulting set contains every label admitted by the localized rule, with additional protection confined to validation-identified classes. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support. Under exchangeability among accepted examples within each protected class, CALCoDe provides finite-sample coverage at the prespecified guard level and contains the corresponding localized conformal sets; coverage on shifted external cohorts is evaluated empirically. Among standard conformal baselines and recent VLM-specific conformal methods evaluated across two dermatology shifts (HAM10000 to ISIC 2019 and HAM10000 to PAD-UFES-20) and four frozen VLM backbones (BiomedCLIP, OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32, PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32, and MedSigLIP-448), CALCoDe is the only approach whose observed marginal and worst-class accepted coverage both reach 0.95 in all eight settings. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, its average worst-class accepted coverage is 0.970, compared with 0.926 for sTACP and 0.864 for LCP-VLM.

</details>


### [138] [Flow Matching with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28698)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We propose Missing-Data Flow Matching, which handles missing coordinates in training data by treating them as latent variables and averaging the flow matching loss over possible completions. We prove that under MCAR, the incomplete-data objective equals the complete-data objective exactly. Finite-sample analysis reveals that missingness transfers estimator variance rather than adding it, one completion per example matches complete-data variance, and under a fixed evaluation budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes a single irreducible bias bounded by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance. Experiments confirm that deterministic imputation collapses the generated distribution and validate the theoretical predictions on real tabular data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching assumes fully observed training data, but many real-world datasets contain missing values. Existing methods cannot directly handle missing data in flow matching, motivating the need for a principled approach.

Method: Missing-Data Flow Matching treats missing coordinates as latent variables and averages the flow matching loss over possible completions. The correction is proven exact under MCAR. Finite-sample analysis answers design questions: missingness transfers variance, one completion per example matches complete-data variance, and under fixed budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes bias bounded by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance to the true completion law.

Result: Theoretical proofs show exactness of the correction under MCAR. Finite-sample analysis provides counterintuitive insights: missingness transfers variance rather than adding it, one completion suffices for variance matching, and one completion is optimal under fixed budget. Learned completion models add irreducible bias bounded by Wasserstein distance. Experiments validate that deterministic imputation leads to distribution collapse, while the proposed method performs competitively with strong imputation baselines on real tabular data.

Conclusion: Missing-Data Flow Matching provides a theoretically grounded method for handling missing data in flow matching, with exact correction under MCAR and practical insights about completion strategies. The method is effective on real tabular data and avoids collapse caused by deterministic imputation.

Abstract: Flow matching assumes fully observed training data, which many real-world applications rarely provide. We propose Missing-Data Flow Matching, which treats the missing coordinates of training samples as latent variables and averages the flow matching loss over the values they could take. We first prove the correction is exact rather than approximate. Under missing completely at random with true completions, the incomplete-data objective equals the complete-data objective, so missingness changes nothing about what flow matching learns and the entire difficulty relocates to the completion model. Our finite-sample analysis then answers design questions that the algorithm leaves open, and the answers are not the ones intuition suggests. Missingness transfers estimator variance rather than adding it, one completion per example already matches complete-data variance exactly, and under a fixed evaluation budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes a single irreducible bias, which we bound by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance to the true completion law. Experiments numerically validate the theoretical predictions, show that deterministic rather than frozen imputation is what collapses the generated distribution, and place our method alongside strong classical and deep imputation baselines on real tabular data.

</details>


### [139] [MMFGU: Multimodal Federated Graph Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28708)
*Haodong Lu,Zekai Chen,Weiwei Ji,Shihao Li,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MMFGU，一种多模态联邦图遗忘框架，通过目标特定表示解耦，支持细粒度、异构遗忘请求（如删除特定图像/文本/交互），同时保留其他内容，防止通过剩余模态恢复信息，并阻断跨客户端的痕迹传播，达到41.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图遗忘仅支持实体/关系或客户移除，无法满足多模态场景下更频繁和细粒度的遗忘请求（如仅删除某张图片或文本）。需要处理三个挑战：只移除请求信息不损害保留内容；防止通过剩余模态或图邻域恢复目标；阻止相关痕迹在其他客户端上重新进入全局模型。

Method: MMFGU框架围绕目标特定表示解耦：将异构请求映射为统一目标载体；解耦请求表示同时锚定保留语义；用轻量探针暴露并修复传播残差；通过紧凑原型和响应信号选择性清除受影响客户端。

Result: 有效移除请求信息，保留图数据效用，相比完整重训练实现41.5倍加速。

Conclusion: MMFGU能够高效处理多模态联邦学习中的细粒度遗忘请求，保护隐私并保持模型性能，为多模态联邦图遗忘提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning enables clients to collaboratively train graph models over structural, textual, and visual signals without sharing private local data. However, the presence of heterogeneous multimodal content also makes unlearning requests more frequent and fine-grained: users may delete accounts or interactions, remove a particular image or text while retaining the associated entity, or revoke the learned correspondence between retained modalities or graph attributes. Existing federated graph unlearning mainly handles entity/relation or client removal and cannot directly satisfy these multimodal requests. They introduce three challenges: removing only the requested information without damaging retained content, preventing the target from being recovered through remaining modalities or graph neighborhoods, and stopping related traces on other clients from re-entering the global model after aggregation. To address them, we propose \textsc{\textbf{MMFGU}}, a multimodal federated graph unlearning framework built around target-specific representation decoupling. \textsc{MMFGU} maps heterogeneous requests into unified target carriers, decouples requested representations while anchoring retained semantics, exposes and repairs propagated residuals with lightweight probes, and selectively purges affected clients through compact prototype and response signals. Experiments show that \textsc{MMFGU} effectively removes requested information, preserves retained graph utility, and achieves a $\boldsymbol{41.5\times}$ speedup over full retraining.

</details>


### [140] [Mirror Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28737)
*Yunpeng Liu,Matthew Niedoba,Oluwanifemi A. Adekanye,Jason Yoo,Yingchen He,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出镜像学习（Mirror Learning）框架，通过结合学习到的视角转换（微调视频扩散模型）和逆动力学模型，从第三人称观测生成第一人称专家数据（镜像数据），用于训练模仿学习策略，从而摆脱对密集遥操作数据采集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）在密集且对齐良好的第一人称数据下表现优异，但无法有效利用人类和动物常规利用的第三人称观测信号。当前需要一种能从被动观察中获取可执行策略的方法，以降低对遥操作数据采集的依赖，实现更安全、可扩展的模仿学习。

Method: 提出镜像学习框架，包含两个组件：(i) 学习到的视角转换——使用微调的视频扩散模型将学习者置于演示者的视角；(ii) 逆动力学模型——在学习者的控制空间中推断动作轨迹。通过这两个组件合成镜像数据，即从第三人称观测生成的伪第一人称专家数据。

Result: 实验表明：仅使用镜像数据就能训练出有效策略；将镜像数据与第一人称BC训练数据结合使用可进一步提升下游策略性能。

Conclusion: 现代生成式世界模型隐式编码了足够的结构信息，使得镜像学习能够成为一种可扩展且安全的替代方案，减少对遥操作数据采集的依赖。

Abstract: We investigate imitation learning through the lens of third-person observation and propose a framework for mirror learning: acquiring actionable policies from passive observation. While behavior cloning (BC) excels under dense, well-aligned first-person data, it fundamentally fails to leverage the rich observational signals arising from third-person demonstrations that humans and animals routinely exploit. We introduce a method that composes (i) a learned perspective transformation that places learners in demonstrators' shoes using a fine-tuned video diffusion model and (ii) an inverse dynamics model that infers action trajectories in the learners' control space. This enables the synthesis of mirror data, pseudo first-person expert data generated from third-person observations of demonstrator behavior. Empirically, we show that mirror data alone can train effective policies, and that augmenting first-person BC training with mirror data further improves downstream policy performance. Our results suggest that modern generative world models implicitly encode sufficient structure to enable a scalable and safe alternative to teleoperation-heavy data collection.

</details>


### [141] [TAGTorch: A PyTorch Library for Geometry, Topology, and Symmetry-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28755)
*Brendan Kennedy,Tegan Emerson,Gregory Roek,Emilie Purvine,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAGTorch is an open-source PyTorch library that unifies tools from topology, algebra, and geometry for neural network applications, addressing the fragmented software ecosystem in this area.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The increasing application of neural networks to data with geometric, topological, or symmetry-related structure has led to many algorithmic developments, but the supporting software ecosystem remains fragmented with many methods existing only as unmaintained research prototypes.

Method: The authors introduce TAGTorch, an open-source PyTorch-based library that unifies tools inspired by topology, algebra, and geometry, including data-preprocessing methods, architectures, training techniques, and model analysis tools. They describe its design philosophy and current architecture and capabilities.

Result: TAGTorch provides a unified library that fills gaps in the current software ecosystem for geometric, topological, and algebraic deep learning.

Conclusion: The authors discuss future development priorities for the library, indicating ongoing work to expand its capabilities.

Abstract: Over the last decade, neural networks have been applied to an increasingly diverse range of applications, including data with rich geometric, topological, or symmetry-related structure. As a result, researchers have increasingly drawn inspiration from topology, algebra, and geometry. Despite this rich algorithmic development, the supporting software ecosystem remains fragmented. Many important methods exist only as research prototypes in unmaintained repositories. We address this by introducing Topology, Algebra, and Geometry Torch (TAGTorch), an open-source, PyTorch-based library that unifies tools inspired by topology, algebra, and geometry, including data-preprocessing methods, architectures, training techniques, and model analysis tools. We describe the design philosophy of TAGTorch and then discuss its current architecture and capabilities, highlighting areas where it can fill gaps in the current software ecosystem. We conclude with a discussion of our future development priorities for the library.

</details>


### [142] [Feature Interaction Modeling for Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28762)
*Quan Gu,Hongxia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将因子分解机（FM）中的特征交互模块嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINN）和神经算子学习中，以提高对参数化偏微分方程（PDE）解流形的表达能力。提出了FM-PINN，显式捕获时空变量交互以提高光滑高阶PDE的近似精度。进一步将空间坐标、时间、物理参数及初边值条件分组，建模跨组交互，开发了FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet，在非线性守恒律和具有尖锐梯度或不连续性的问题上尤其有效。数值测试表明，该方法在激波主导方程上取得了显著的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN和神经算子方法在表达参数化PDE解流形时能力有限，特别是对于具有强交叉场依赖的问题（如激波、不连续性）。受多元函数二阶泰勒展开表征变量耦合的启发，希望引入显式的特征交互机制来增强模型表达能力。

Method: 首先提出FM-PINN，利用因子分解机风格的二阶特征交互层捕获时空变量之间的相互作用。进一步将输入变量（空间坐标、时间、物理参数、初边值条件）划分为独立特征集，并建模跨组交互，从而构建FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet。这些模型在保持物理一致性的同时，显式地建模了变量之间的高阶耦合关系。

Result: FM-PINN在光滑高阶PDE上提高了近似精度；FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet在非线性守恒律、激波等具有尖锐梯度或不连续性的问题上取得了显著的精度增益，而在光滑算子学习基准上无一致优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的特征交互机制为物理一致性建模参数化PDE提供了有前景的方向，特别适用于具有强交叉场依赖的挑战性问题。该方法显式建模变量交互，能有效处理激波主导方程等困难场景。

Abstract: This work embeds feature interaction modules derived from factorization machines (FMs) into physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and neural operator learning, to enhance model expressiveness for solution manifolds of parameterized partial differential equations (PDEs). Motivated by the second-order Taylor expansion of multivariate functions to characterize variable couplings, we first propose FM-PINN. It explicitly captures spatio-temporal variable interactions and improves the approximation accuracy for smooth high-order PDEs. We further group spatial coordinates, time, physical parameters, and initial and boundary conditions into independent feature sets and model their cross-group interactions. Based on this strategy, we develop FM-Operator and FM-DeepONet, which are particularly effective for nonlinear conservation laws and problems with sharp gradients or discontinuities, while offering no consistent advantage on smooth operator learning benchmarks. Numerical tests demonstrate that the proposed mechanism delivers substantial accuracy gains on challenging shock-dominated equations, indicating a promising direction for physics-consistent modeling of parameterized PDEs with strong cross-field dependencies.

</details>


### [143] [Representations from Pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials as Coarse Coordinates for Material Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28776)
*Paul Hagemann,Katharina Ueltzen,Simon Müller,Janine George,Philipp Benner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练机器学习原子间势（如MACE）的原子平均特征来改进无机晶体结构生成模型的评估与引导。引入了一种基于分布的距离度量——粗细输运距离（CFTD），该度量通过粗粒度MACE特征衡量结构质量并检测记忆化，同时表明粗粒度MACE特征可用于指导材料生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型及其评估方法依赖于简单的结构表示，缺乏能够同时捕捉质量和新颖性的统一评估框架，也未能充分利用预训练原子间势的丰富特征表示。

Method: 1) 提出CFTD距离度量，基于两种不同特征提取器，其中质量分量使用粗粒度MACE特征，将质量与新颖性纳入单一分布评估框架；2) 将粗粒度MACE特征作为生成模型的引导信号。

Result: CFTD能有效捕捉晶体结构质量并检测记忆化，与连续SUN指标对比展示了其通用性；粗粒度MACE特征成功用于指导材料生成模型。

Conclusion: 预训练MLIP的原子平均特征（如MACE）在晶体结构生成模型的评估和引导方面具有显著优势，CFTD提供了一种更全面的质量-新颖性评估方法。

Abstract: Generative machine learning is increasingly used for inorganic crystal structure generation. Most models and the corresponding evaluation approaches rely on simple forms of crystal structure representation. In this paper, we showcase the power of atom-averaged features from pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs), such as MACE, for such tasks. We first introduce a distance measure that assesses the output of material generative models by capturing both quality and novelty in a single distribution-based evaluation framework. In particular, we introduce the Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD) using two different featurizers, where the quality component is based on coarse MACE features. We showcase CFTD's versatility in capturing crystal-structure quality while also detecting memorization, and compare it with the recently introduced continuous SUN metrics. We further show that coarse MACE features can be used as guidance for a material generative model.

</details>


### [144] [Distilling Knowledge from Large Language Models into Lightweight Reinforcement Learning Agents for Autonomous Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28826)
*Konur Tholl,François Rivest,Mariam El Mezouar,Adrian Taylor,Ranwa Al Mallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将大型语言模型（LLM）用于自主网络防御决策，通过提示工程而非微调，证明一个80亿参数的网络安全预训练LLM能在CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境中优于基线强化学习（RL）代理。进而提出一种在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM的防御策略迁移到仅含64,910参数的轻量级RL代理中，大幅减少模型规模的同时保持有效防御能力。实验评估了不同网络配置下的迁移性以及教师引导的RL稳定策略，发现均未能持续超越教师策略，表明奖励驱动的RL优化与教师引导防御策略之间存在对齐局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络操作（ACO）中常用的强化学习代理需要大量探索训练，导致行为不稳定和初期决策能力差。本文旨在利用大型语言模型（LLM）来改善自主防御决策，并通过策略蒸馏将LLM的能力迁移至轻量级代理，以实现实际部署。

Method: 采用提示工程而非微调的方式，使用一个80亿参数的网络安全预训练LLM在修改后的CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境中进行防御决策。提出在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM的防御策略迁移至一个仅有64,910参数的轻量级RL代理。构建了4到12台主机的CybORG场景，评估不同网络配置下的迁移性，并测试了教师引导的RL稳定策略。

Result: LLM在防御决策上优于基线RL代理；通过策略蒸馏，轻量级代理在保持有效防御能力的同时模型规模大幅减小；教师引导的RL稳定策略未能持续超越原始的教师策略，表明存在策略对齐局限。

Conclusion: 以网络安全为重点的LLM可以作为自主网络防御的专家知识源，而策略蒸馏为将前沿网络安全模型部署到高效、可扩展的轻量级代理中提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Cyber Operations (ACO) are increasingly important for defending enterprise networks as cyber threats continue to evolve in sophistication. ACO applications commonly employ Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents to learn defensive behaviors through interaction with environments. However, RL agents typically require extensive exploration during training, often resulting in unstable behavior and poor initial decision-making before converging toward effective defense strategies.
  In this work, we investigate the use of a Large Language Model (LLM) to improve autonomous defensive decision-making within an ACO environment. Through prompt engineering rather than fine-tuning, we demonstrate that an 8-billion parameter LLM pretrained on cybersecurity data can outperform a baseline RL agent in a modified CybORG CAGE Challenge 2 environment. We then propose an online policy distillation framework that transfers the LLM's defensive policy into a lightweight RL agent containing only 64,910 parameters, reducing model size by several orders of magnitude while maintaining effective defensive capabilities. This provides a pathway toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within lightweight, deployable agents.
  To evaluate transferability, we construct CybORG scenarios ranging from 4 to 12 hosts and assess the approach across varying network configurations. We also evaluate teacher-guided RL stabilization strategies and observe that none consistently surpass the optimized teacher policy, suggesting policy-alignment limitations between reward-driven RL optimization and teacher-guided defense strategies.
  Our results demonstrate the potential of cybersecurity-focused LLMs as sources of expertise for autonomous cyber defense, while policy distillation provides a practical path toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within efficient, scalable agents.

</details>


### [145] [Hypergradient-based Bilevel Reinforcement Learning with Improved Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28849)
*Naman Saxena,Mudit Gaur,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超梯度的双层强化学习算法，利用熵正则化折扣RL目标下玻尔兹曼策略的最优性，实现了无海森矩阵计算、迭代复杂度O(ε^{-1})和最先进的样本复杂度Õ(ε^{-2})，并去除了外层目标函数的Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层RL算法要么因使用含海森矩阵的超梯度而不可扩展，要么因使用基于惩罚的近似方法而样本复杂度高。本文旨在设计一种既高效可扩展又具有低样本复杂度的双层RL算法。

Method: 利用熵正则化折扣RL目标函数下玻尔兹曼策略的最优性，提出一种无海森矩阵的超梯度双层RL算法。该算法通过玻尔兹曼策略的解析形式避免直接计算海森矩阵，从而降低计算负担。

Result: 在温和正则性条件下，算法达到了迭代复杂度O(ε^{-1})和样本复杂度Õ(ε^{-2})，且收敛分析中无需外层目标函数满足Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件，超越了先前最先进的工作。

Conclusion: 该工作为双层RL提供了一种高效、可扩展且理论保证更强的算法，有望推动元学习、层次任务分解和基于人类反馈的强化学习等应用的发展。

Abstract: Bilevel reinforcement learning (RL) is an important framework within the literature of RL that can be used to formalize various categories of problems, such as meta-learning, hierarchical task decomposition, and reinforcement learning from human feedback (RL-HF). Most of the bilevel RL algorithms are either not scalable because of using hypergradient with Hessian, or they suffer from high sample complexity because of using penalty-based approximation methods. In this work, we propose a hypergradient-based bilevel RL algorithm using the optimality of the Boltzmann policy for the entropy regularized discounted RL objective function. Our proposed algorithm is Hessian-free and obtains an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-1})$ and state-of-the-art sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ under mild regularity conditions. Further, in our convergence analysis, we are able to remove the assumption of the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition on the outer-level objective function present in the prior state-of-the-art sample complexity work.

</details>


### [146] [An analysis of machine learning approaches for enhancing decision-making in complex discrete choice tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28854)
*Sheng Lun Christine Cao,Destenie Nock,Alex Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种机器学习模型（多项逻辑回归、广义加性模型、孪生神经网络和高斯过程）在学习五种离散选择规则（线性强效用、单调强效用、理想点、词典半序和多属性线性弹道累积器）上的能力。通过蒙特卡洛实验和真实能源政策偏好案例研究，发现半参数和非参数模型通常优于参数模型，性能随训练集和确定性提高而改善，并指出选择任务环境应驱动模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散选择建模常用于政策制定中的偏好诱导，但传统上采用参数模型。机器学习可推动该领域发展，然而关于机器学习方法在个体异质性下估计离散选择规则的能力知之甚少，尤其是在偏好诱导的挑战背景下。因此需要系统评估机器学习模型的学习与预测能力。

Method: 评估四种机器学习模型：多项逻辑回归（参数）、广义加性模型（半参数）、孪生神经网络（非参数）和高斯过程（非参数）。通过蒙特卡洛实验，改变属性数量、训练集数量和选择规则确定性，并测试五种选择规则（线性强效用、单调强效用、理想点、词典半序、多属性线性弹道累积器）。最后使用真实能源政策偏好数据进行案例研究。

Result: 半参数和非参数模型在所有实验设定下普遍优于参数模型。模型性能随训练集增加提升6%-96%，随规则确定性提升0%-55%。案例研究中，孪生神经网络（TNN）表现最佳，BIC为13.351。

Conclusion: 证明了半参数和非参数模型在政策导向的离散选择建模中的可行性与局限性，并表明选择任务环境应驱动模型选择。

Abstract: Discrete choice modeling is a common tool used for preference elicitation during policy-making, but this is typically done through parametric models. Machine learning can push the boundaries of discrete choice modeling for policy-based preference elicitation by adopting a data-driven approach or learning individual preferences. However, there is limited knowledge of how well machine learning methods can estimate individual discrete choice rules under individual heterogeneity, especially in the context of challenges often experienced during preference elicitation. This study evaluates four machine learning models (multinomial logistic regression, generalized additive model, twinned neural network, and Gaussian process) with respect to their capacity to learn and predict five choice rules that are important in the behavioral and social sciences (linear strong utility, monotonic strong utility, ideal point, lexicographic semiorder, and multiattribute linear ballistic accumulator). Monte Carlo experiments were performed to assess model performance when increasing a) the number of attributes in the choice alternatives, b) the number of training choice sets, and c) the choice rule's determinism. The simulation results demonstrated that semi-parametric and non-parametric models generally outperform parametric models across all choice rules and experimental contexts. Model performance also generally improves by 6% to 96% and 0% to 55%, respectively, with an increase in training choice sets and choice rule determinism. A case study using real energy policy preference data was also conducted, where TNN performed best with a BIC of 13.351. This work demonstrated the viability and limitations of semi-parametric and non-parametric models in the context of policy-centric discrete choice modeling and showed how the choice task context should drive model selection.

</details>


### [147] [Fast Rates for Swap-Agnostic Learning of Proper Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28856)
*Princewill Okoroafor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“交换无关学习”（swap-agnostic learning）框架，它允许比较器在学习者预测的每个水平集上选择不同的假设，从而捕捉依赖于预测的后处理。主要结果表明，对于任何固定的适当损失（proper loss），可以实现离线交换无关学习，其过量风险为 $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$，在线交换遗憾为 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$。同时，对于整个损失族，也给出了相应的速率，并改进了之前 Luo 等人（2025）的 $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3}(\log |H|)^{1/3})$ 速率。核心技术贡献是将交换无关学习归约到一种二阶形式的多校准（multicalibration），通过 Blackwell 可接近性和 Bernstein 风格的方差修正实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的不可知学习（agnostic learning）中，比较器只能选择一个固定的假设。交换无关学习允许比较器在每个预测水平集上选择不同的假设，这更适用于需要预测相关后处理（如决策或分组公平性）的场景。然而，这似乎需要为每个可能的预测值解决一个独立的不可知学习问题。本文旨在证明对于适当损失，这些逐预测的比较可以联合控制，从而设计出高效的交换无关学习算法。

Method: 通过 Blackwell 可接近性（Blackwell approachability）结合 Bernstein 风格的方差修正，将交换无关学习归约到一种二阶形式的多校准（second-order multicalibration）。这种归约使得原本需要逐预测值分别处理的问题转变为全局联合优化问题，从而简化了学习过程。

Result: 1. 对于任何固定的光滑适当损失，离线过量风险为 $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$，在线交换遗憾为 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$。2. 对于所有有界于 $[-1,1]$ 的适当损失，在线和离线速率分别为 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T\log |H|})$ 和 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\log |H|/m})$。3. 对于凸的、1-Lipschitz 的适当损失，速率改进为 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$（在线）和 $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$（离线）。这些界在忽略对数因子下是紧的，并且优于 Luo 等人（2025）的 $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3}(\log |H|)^{1/3})$ 速率。

Conclusion: 本文建立了交换无关学习与多校准之间的深层联系，通过方差校正的 Blackwell 可接近性实现了更优的速率。所提出的算法同时适用于单个损失和整个损失族，且获得的界在理论上是最优的（除对数因子外）。这为预测后处理和公平性学习提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Swap-agnostic learning strengthens classical agnostic learning by allowing the comparator to select a different hypothesis on each level set of the learner's predictions. This benchmark captures prediction-dependent postprocessing, but appears to require solving a separate agnostic-learning problem for every possible prediction value. We show that, for proper losses, these prediction-level comparisons can instead be controlled jointly. Our main result is an offline swap-agnostic learner for any fixed proper loss. For a finite hypothesis class $H$ and any fixed smooth proper loss, the excess risk from $m$ i.i.d. samples is $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$, with a corresponding online swap-regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$. We also give algorithms whose predictions are simultaneously swap-agnostic for entire families of losses. For all proper losses bounded in $[-1,1]$, we obtain online and offline rates of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T\log |H|})$ and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\log |H|/m})$, respectively. For convex, $1$-Lipschitz proper losses, these rates improve to $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$ online and $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$ offline. These bounds are tight up to logarithmic factors and improve upon the $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3}(\log |H|)^{1/3})$ rate implied by the swap-omniprediction guarantee of Luo et al. (2025). Our main technical contribution is a reduction from swap-agnostic learning to a second-order form of multicalibration, obtained via Blackwell approachability with a Bernstein-style variance correction.

</details>


### [148] [Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28902)
*Bin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种并行框架，通过组装静态梯度方法实现更好的自适应性。静态梯度方法 GD(x0,T) 使用预定步长函数 S(T) 执行固定迭代次数。并行框架使用 p 个处理器按照几何序列并行搜索合适的 T，使得梯度下降满足期望的收敛条件。每个处理器在无限阶段序列中执行 GD(x0, T_{j,i})，其中 T_{j,i}=h(j,i) 为预设函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态梯度方法需要手动选择迭代次数 T，缺乏自适应性。为了自动确定合适的 T 以满足收敛条件，提出并行框架来搜索 T，提高算法的自适应性和效率。

Method: 定义静态梯度方法 GD(x0,T)，步长 s=S(T) 为 T 的预定函数，迭代格式为 x_{i+1}=x_i - (η/s)·g_i。并行框架包含 p 个处理器，每个处理器执行无限阶段序列；阶段 i 中处理器 j 被分配 T_{j,i}=h(j,i)（h 为预设函数），并执行 GD(x0, T_{j,i})。通过几何序列搜索合适的 T 使得梯度下降满足收敛条件。

Result: 该并行框架能够自动搜索合适的迭代次数 T，使得梯度下降满足期望的收敛条件，从而实现更好的自适应性。

Conclusion: 提出的并行框架有效组装静态梯度方法，通过并行搜索自动调整迭代次数，克服了手动选择 T 的不足，提高了算法的自适应性和收敛性能。

Abstract: We develop a parallel framework that assembles static gradient methods to achieve better adaptivity. A static gradient method, denoted by $\mathrm{GD}(x_0,T)$, takes as input an initial point $x_0\in\mathbb{R}^n$ and $T\in \mathbb{R}^+$ specifying the number $\floor{T}$ of iterations. The step size is chosen as $s=S(T)$, where $S(\cdot)$ is a predetermined function of $T$. The method then performs the iterations $ x_{i+1}=x_i-\fracη{s}\cdot g_i,$ where $g_i$ is a stochastic gradient evaluated at $x_i$, and $η$ is a scaling factor. For an integer $p\ge1$, the $p$ processors in the proposed parallel framework search for an appropriate value of $T$ according to a geometric sequence so that the resulting gradient descent satisfies the desired convergence conditions. Each processor executes an infinite sequence of stages indexed by $i=1,2,\ldots$. At stage $i$, processor $j$ is assigned $ T_{j,i}=h(j,i),$ where $h:\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N} \rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}$ is a prescribed function. Processor $j$ $(j=0,1,\ldots,p-1)$ executes $\mathrm{GD}(x_0, T_{j,i})$ at stage $i$.

</details>


### [149] [Reflection or Re-Generation? Why LLM Revision Fails Where Human Revision Succeeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28908)
*Yefan Tao,Gerald Friedland,Madhusudhanan Chandrasekaran,Luyang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了人类-LLM反思框架（HRF），通过信息论分析比较人类和LLM的反思行为。发现LLM在客观任务上反思几乎无信息增益（≈0），在主观任务上产生负增益，而人类反思均有正增益。结论是LLM反思本质上是条件性重新生成，而非真正的错误驱动修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地要求“反思”，但其是否类似人类修订仍不清楚。本文旨在系统比较人类和LLM在相同条件下的反思行为，探究LLM反思的有效性。

Method: 引入HRF（人类-LLM反思框架），采用两轮协议（two-pass protocol），在自我、同伴和跨智能体设置下控制条件比较人类与LLM的修订。基于每轮交叉熵减少的信息论分析，并设计诊断分析（基于首轮正确性的条件修订、基于oracle错误信号的修订与随机重排基线对比）。

Result: 客观任务上LLM反思信息增益接近零（ΔI≈0），相当于无信息重新采样；主观任务上产生显著负增益（ΔI<0），使预测远离目标。人类反思在两种任务上均为正增益。跨智能体实验表明失败源于修订步骤本身而非输入质量：LLM甚至降低高质量人类回答的质量。诊断分析显示自错误检测在客观多选题中存在但在主观任务中弱；基于oracle信号的恢复在某些模型上超过基线，在某些模型上低于基线。

Conclusion: LLM反思并非真正的错误驱动修订，而是条件性重新生成（conditioned re-generation），因为缺乏外部信息时自我条件化修订无法降低关于目标的不确定性。

Abstract: Reflection, the ability to revisit and revise prior reasoning, is central to how humans improve their answers. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly prompted to "reflect," yet whether this resembles human revision remains unclear. We introduce the Human-LLM Reflection Framework (HRF), a controlled two-pass protocol comparing human and LLM revision under identical conditions across self-, peer-, and cross-agent settings. Using an information-theoretic analysis based on per-iteration cross-entropy reduction, we find two failure modes of LLM reflection. On objective tasks with finite answer spaces, reflection yields near-zero information gain (Delta I approx 0), behaving as neutral re-generation indistinguishable from re-sampling. On subjective tasks, it yields significant negative gain (Delta I < 0), moving predictions away from the target. Human revision, by contrast, yields positive gain in both settings. Cross-agent experiments localize the failure to the revision step, not input quality: LLMs degrade even high-quality human responses. Diagnostic analyses (revision conditioned on first-pass correctness, and oracle-guided revision against a random-reshuffle baseline) show that which sub-step dominates varies by task and by model rather than reducing to a single mechanism: self-error detection is present on objective multiple-choice tasks but weak on subjective ones, and recovery under an oracle error signal exceeds the baseline for some models and falls below it for others. The unifying account is structural: without external information, self-conditioned revision cannot reduce uncertainty about the target, so LLM reflection is better understood as conditioned re-generation than as genuine error-driven revision.

</details>


### [150] [Gated Q-learning: Add Off-Policy Bias to Taste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28916)
*Brett Daley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gated Q-learning introduces a continuous gating mechanism to smoothly interpolate between Watkins' and Peng's Q(λ), eliminating the binary choice between bias elimination and faster learning, enabling longer credit-assignment horizons with reduced off-policy bias.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multistep credit assignment is critical for sample-efficient RL, but managing off-policy bias in Q-learning has been a fundamental challenge. Practitioners were limited to a binary choice: eliminate bias at the cost of truncated eligibility traces (Watkins' Q(λ)) or ignore bias to learn faster while injecting errors (Peng's Q(λ)). Modern off-policy estimators fail due to importance-sampling ratios collapsing under Q-learning's greedy target policy.

Method: Gated Q-learning employs a continuous, state-action-dependent gating mechanism to selectively attenuate eligibility traces in an exploration-aware manner, rather than relying on importance sampling. It smoothly interpolates between Watkins' and Peng's Q(λ) extremes.

Result: Empirical evaluations verify that intermediate gating safely enables longer credit-assignment horizons, yielding faster initial learning than either extreme. The expected operator remains a contraction mapping with a provable fixed point.

Conclusion: Gated Q-learning offers a simple alternative to importance sampling while enabling customization of the effective multistep horizon and the amount of off-policy bias in Q-learning agents, ending the long-standing dilemma.

Abstract: Multistep credit assignment is critical for sample-efficient reinforcement learning, yet managing off-policy bias in Q-learning remains a fundamental challenge. For 30 years, practitioners have been limited to a binary choice: eliminate the bias at the cost of severely truncated eligibility traces (Watkins' Q($λ$)), or ignore the bias to learn faster while injecting detrimental errors into the value estimates (Peng's Q($λ$)). Modern off-policy estimators fail to resolve this tension, as importance-sampling ratios collapse under Q-learning's greedy target policy. We introduce Gated Q-learning, a novel algorithmic framework that ends this dilemma by smoothly interpolating between the two historical extremes. Rather than relying on importance sampling, our approach employs a continuous, state-action-dependent gating mechanism to selectively attenuate eligibility traces in an exploration-aware manner. We provide a rigorous theoretical foundation for this mechanism, proving that the expected operator remains a contraction mapping and deriving its exact fixed point. Empirical evaluations verify that intermediate gating safely enables longer credit-assignment horizons, yielding faster initial learning than either extreme. Gated Q-learning offers a simple alternative to importance sampling while enabling customization of the effective multistep horizon and the amount of off-policy bias in Q-learning agents.

</details>


### [151] [Learning Optimal Dynamic Matching via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28925)
*Genta Okada,Shunya Noda,Junpei Komiyama,Akira Matsushita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对动态匹配市场中的"匹配时机"问题，提出了一个基于价值学习的强化学习框架。该框架在有限、演化的加权图上建模，利用事件时间约简将决策时刻限制为外生事件之后，并进一步证明最优边级Q函数可由单个残差图上的延续价值函数表示。通过图神经网络近似残差图价值并使用时序差分学习训练，结合前向贪心匹配启发式进行动作选择。在二元类型基准和肾脏配对捐赠基准上的实验表明，该方法显著优于即时匹配和阈值贪心规则，能够根据实时的连通性和退出信息自适应调整匹配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态匹配市场需要在"立即匹配获得价值"与"保留参与者以创造未来更好机会"之间权衡。现有方法往往依赖简单的启发式规则（如即时匹配或阈值贪心），无法充分利用市场的动态结构信息。核心动机是开发一个能够学习状态依赖的动态匹配策略的框架，使得匹配决策能够自适应地根据实时的图连通性和参与者退出信息进行调整。

Method: 1. 建立无限时域连续时间模型，包含随机到达、节点类型转换、边实现和外生退出。2. 证明事件时间约简：最优策略下，规划者只需在每个外生事件发生后立即行动，然后等待下一个事件。3. 证明最优边级Q函数可由单个残差图上的延续价值函数表征，将学习对象从状态-动作值降维为图价值。4. 使用图神经网络近似残差图价值，通过时序差分学习训练。5. 采用前向贪心匹配启发式进行动作选择（因为精确动作选择仍需组合优化）。

Result: 在二元类型基准中，学习到的策略显著优于即时匹配和阈值贪心规则：它能保留通用节点以等待稀有而有价值的匹配机会，仅在节点池充裕时形成低价值匹配。在肾脏配对捐赠基准中：当退出不可预测时性能与即时贪心相当；当退出预警可靠时恢复了患者匹配的逻辑；在中间预警概率区间上优于即时贪心和耐心贪心中较好的一个。

Conclusion: 残差图价值学习能够产生状态依赖的动态匹配策略，这些策略能够根据实时的连通性和退出信息自适应调整。该方法为动态匹配市场中"匹配时机"问题提供了一种新的解决方案，证明了将强化学习与图表示学习结合的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic matching markets require decisions about whom to match and when: matching now yields value but removes participants who may create better future opportunities. We develop a value-based reinforcement-learning framework for this problem on finite, evolving weighted graphs. We study an infinite-horizon continuous-time model with stochastic arrivals, node-type transitions, edge realizations, and exogenous exits. We prove an event-time reduction: without loss of optimality, the planner acts immediately after each exogenous event and then waits for the next one. We further show that the optimal edge-wise $Q$-function is characterized by a single continuation-value function on post-decision residual graphs, reducing the learned object from state-action values to graph values. Exact action selection still requires combinatorial matching optimization; we approximate the value with a graph neural network, train it by temporal-difference learning, and use it in a forward-greedy matching heuristic. In a binary-type benchmark, the learned policy substantially outperforms immediate and threshold-greedy rules by preserving common nodes for rare arrivals of valuable matches while forming lower-value matches only in thick pools. In a kidney paired donation benchmark, it performs similarly to immediate greedy when exits are unpredictable, recovers the logic of patient matching when warnings are reliable, and outperforms the better of Immediate Greedy and Patient Greedy across intermediate warning probabilities. These results show that residual-graph value learning yields state-dependent dynamic matching policies that adapt to realized connectivity and exit information.

</details>


### [152] [Latent Lie-Poisson Neural Networks (LLPNNs): Discovering the motion of Lie-Poisson systems through observable data and latent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28939)
*Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Lie--Poisson 神经网络 (LLPNNs)，一种从可观测数据直接学习 Lie--Poisson 动力学（适用于具有对称性的哈密顿系统）的保结构框架；通过结合哈密顿解码器/伪拉格朗日编码器、Noether 不变量和 Magnus 李群更新，在动量变量不可观测的情况下实现长期精确预测，并适用于正则和退化哈密顿系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重要的哈密顿系统（如刚体、水下航行器、流体、等离子体及最优控制问题）具有对称性，可约化为 Lie--Poisson 系统，但其动力学涉及通常不可观测的动量变量，而可用数据仅为位形和速度等可观测量。在最优控制中，潜在变量还包含不可观的协态，且哈密顿量可能是退化的，导致无法构造对应的拉格朗日量，使得编码器-解码器方法不再适用。因此需要一种能从可观测数据直接学习潜在 Lie--Poisson 动力学的保结构方法。

Method: LLPNNs 利用三个几何要素：(i) 学习主动变量上的哈密顿解码器或伪拉格朗日编码器；(ii) 通过 Lie--Poisson 对称性约化产生的普遍 Noether 不变量构造潜在轨迹；(iii) 结合基于 Magnus 的李群更新，通过 Lie--Poisson 流重构可观测量和潜在动力学。该方法保持几何结构，适用于正则和退化哈密顿系统。

Result: 在 SO(3) 上的广义刚体、SE(3) 上的 Kirchhoff 水下航行器以及 SE(2)^N 上的交互车辆最优控制问题三个示例上进行了验证。数值实验表明，该方法具有出色的长期预测准确性、强鲁棒性（抗噪声），且仅需适中的数据集和轻量级神经网络架构即可获得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: LLPNNs 提供了一种有效的保结构学习框架，能够从可观测数据中学习 Lie--Poisson 动力学，克服了动量变量不可观测和哈密顿退化带来的挑战，在多个具有对称性的哈密顿系统上取得了优异的长期预测和鲁棒性表现。

Abstract: Structure-preserving neural networks are essential for the long-term prediction of Hamiltonian systems from data. Many important Hamiltonian systems in mechanics and control admit symmetry reduction to Lie--Poisson systems, including rigid bodies, underwater vehicles, fluids, plasmas, and optimal control problems. A fundamental challenge in learning such systems is that their dynamics evolve in momentum variables that are typically unobservable, while available data consist only of observable quantities such as configurations and velocities. In optimal control applications, the situation is further complicated because the latent variables contain unobservable co-states and the Hamiltonian may be degenerate, preventing the existence of a corresponding Lagrangian and rendering the encoder-decoder approaches inapplicable.
  We introduce Latent Lie--Poisson Neural Networks (LLPNNs), a structure-preserving framework for learning Lie--Poisson dynamics directly from observable data. The proposed approach exploits three geometric ingredients: (i) learning either a Hamiltonian decoder or a pseudo-Lagrangian encoder on the active variables, (ii) constructing latent trajectories through a universal Noether invariant arising from Lie--Poisson symmetry reduction, and (iii) reconstructing observable and latent dynamics through Lie--Poisson flows combined with Magnus-based Lie-group updates. The resulting method preserves the geometric structure and is applicable to both regular and degenerate Hamiltonian systems.
  We demonstrate the method on three examples: a generalized rigid body on SO(3), Kirchhoff's underwater vehicle on SE(3), and an optimal-control problem for interacting vehicles on $SE(2)^N$. Numerical experiments show excellent long-term predictive accuracy, strong robustness to noise, and competitive performance using only modest datasets and lightweight neural-network architectures.

</details>


### [153] [FairDiffuseVQVAE: Sampling-Time Fairness in Tabular Diffusion via Conditional Refinement of Vector-Quantized Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28945)
*Nitish Nagesh,Mahdi Bagheri,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairDiffuseVQVAE 是一种两阶段架构，用于生成公平的合成表格数据：第一阶段使用带有行级判别器的向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE），第二阶段使用 DiffuseVAE 风格的连续扩散精炼器，通过分类器无关的引导条件化于第一阶段重建结果和受保护属性。公平性通过在推理时对受保护属性进行均匀采样实现人口统计平价，无需额外的损失项。在 Adult、Bank 和 COMPAS 数据集上，该方法在人口统计平价比（0.702，比 FairTabDDPM 高 47%）和等胜算比（0.686，高 100%）上达到最高，同时保持了最低的成对相关误差（0.034），但以约 15 个 AUC 点为代价换取这些公平性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格扩散模型（如 TabDDPM、TabSyn）在分布保真度上表现出色，但缺乏公平性机制；而现有的公平感知生成器（如 DECAF、FairTGAN、FairTabDDPM）通过在训练时施加显式的公平性惩罚，虽然带来了一定的公平性提升，但严重损害了样本质量或下游效用。因此，需要一种能够在保持高保真度的同时实现公平性的方法。

Method: 提出 FairDiffuseVQVAE，一种两阶段架构：第一阶段是向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）配合行级判别器，不包含任何公平性项；第二阶段是 DiffuseVAE 风格的连续扩散精炼器，通过分类器无关的引导（classifier-free guidance）条件化于第一阶段的潜在重建和受保护属性。公平性并非通过竞争性损失项实现，而是通过在推理时对受保护属性进行均匀采样，使生成数据的受保护属性分布与均匀分布一致，从而在构造上强制实现人口统计平价。

Result: 在 Adult、Bank 和 COMPAS 数据集上，FairDiffuseVQVAE 取得了最高的平均人口统计平价比（0.702，比 FairTabDDPM 提升 47%）和平均等胜算比（0.686，提升 100%），同时达到了所有已发表方法中最低的平均成对相关误差（0.034）。这些公平性提升的代价是约 15 个 AUC 点的下降。

Conclusion: FairDiffuseVQVAE 通过将保真度与公平性解耦，在合成表格数据生成中实现了优于现有方法的公平性指标，同时允许在公平性和效用之间进行可控的权衡。这为隐私保护且公平的合成数据生成提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data is increasingly used in privacy-preserving data sharing, data augmentation, and to mitigate downstream classifier bias. State-of-the-art tabular diffusion models such as TabDDPM and TabSyn achieve excellent distributional fidelity but offer no mechanism for fairness; conversely, fairness-aware tabular generators (DECAF, FairTGAN, FairTabDDPM) impose explicit fairness penalties at training time, yielding modest fairness gains at substantial cost to either sample quality or downstream utility. We introduce FairDiffuseVQVAE, a two-stage architecture that decouples fidelity from fairness: a vector-quantized autoencoder with a row-level discriminator (Stage~1, no fairness terms) is followed by a DiffuseVAE-style continuous diffusion refiner that conditions on both the Stage-1 reconstruction and the protected attribute via classifier-free guidance (Stage~2). Fairness emerges as a property of the sampling distribution -- uniform sampling of the protected attribute at inference time enforces demographic parity by construction, rather than from competing loss terms. On the Adult, Bank and COMPAS datasets, FairDiffuseVQVAE achieves the highest mean Demographic Parity Ratio ($0.702$, $+47\%$ over FairTabDDPM) and Equalized Odds Ratio ($0.686$, $+100\%$). It also attains the lowest mean pair-wise correlation error ($0.034$) of any published method, while explicitly trading $\sim$$15$ AUC points for these fairness gains.

</details>


### [154] [Shapley-Value-Based Feature Attribution for Data Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28946)
*Xinxue,Qu,Francis Bilson Darku,Hong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Shapley值的特征归因框架，在特征层面平衡数据隐私中的披露风险与数据效用，该方法与具体数据掩码方法、统计/机器学习方法及评估指标无关，实验证明能有效降低披露风险同时保持数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据隐私研究大多在数据集层面权衡风险与效用，缺乏在特征层面处理这一权衡的方法；同时，个人数据的广泛使用带来严重的隐私担忧，需要更精细的隐私保护框架。

Method: 提出一个基于Shapley值的公平特征归因框架，从整体视角看待数据掩码，在特征层面而非数据集层面处理披露风险与数据效用的权衡；该框架与具体的数据掩码方法、统计/机器学习方法以及评估指标无关。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能在有效降低披露风险的同时保持数据效用。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据隐私保护提供了一种新的特征级解决方案，具有方法无关性和通用性，有望在实际隐私保护场景中应用。

Abstract: Despite its many benefits, widespread access to individuals' personal data also causes severe privacy concerns for consumers, companies, and policymakers. This study proposes a novel framework that adapts the Shapley-value-based feature attribution approach to the problem domain of data privacy by capturing the two crucial dimensions of data privacy---disclosure risk and data utility. Our proposed framework takes a holistic view of data masking through a fair feature attribution approach based on Shapley values. Different from the existing literature that mostly focuses on the risk-utility tradeoff at the dataset level, the proposed framework addresses the tradeoff at the feature level. Furthermore, the proposed framework is agnostic to data masking methods, statistical and machine learning methods, and data utility and disclosure risk evaluation metrics. Experimental results show that our proposed method can effectively reduce disclosure risk while preserving data utility.

</details>


### [155] [Overcoming the Weakest-Link Effect in LLM-Driven Program Optimization via Heterogeneous Edit Recombination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28947)
*Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,He Zhang,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HERO，一种利用 LLM 进行零阶程序优化的方法。它通过生成多样、不重叠的原子编辑并基于评估分数系统选择组合，解决了“最弱链效应”——即整批编辑中单个有害编辑会抵消所有有益编辑的问题。在算法问题、策略游戏、LLM 智能体系统设计和机器人路径规划等领域，HERO 相比先前基于 LLM 的优化器找到了更高得分的程序，收敛更快且消耗更少 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 程序优化方法依赖文本“梯度”（一阶更新方向），但程序-分数映射复杂时这些估计不可靠。作者认为显式梯度并非必要，LLM 可基于先验知识直接从当前程序提出原子编辑（零阶优化）。然而零阶搜索存在最弱链效应：整体接受或拒绝一批编辑时，单个有害编辑会抵消其他所有编辑的益处。

Method: HERO 提示 LLM 生成多样、不重叠的原子编辑，然后利用评估器分数系统地选择和组合这些编辑，形成连贯的程序改进。这是一种零阶优化策略，避免了不可靠的梯度估计，并通过精细选择组合编辑来克服最弱链效应。

Result: 在算法问题、策略游戏、LLM 智能体系统设计和机器人路径规划等多个领域，HERO 始终能找到更高得分的程序，收敛速度显著快于先前基于 LLM 的优化器，同时消耗更少的 token。

Conclusion: 显式梯度对于有效的程序优化并非必需；利用 LLM 先验知识的零阶优化结合系统性的编辑选择与组合，能更高效地优化程序。HERO 证明了这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to solve complex problems by searching over program space, offering a general paradigm for scientific problems that can be naturally represented and solved as programs. Despite recent progress, identifying effective optimization directions for a candidate program remains challenging. By analogy with automatic differentiation, existing methods typically guide the search using a textual ``gradient'': a first-order update direction expressed as textual edits. Such gradients are inferred either from previously evaluated programs or from LLM-generated feedback on the implicit program-score mapping. However, these estimates become increasingly unreliable as the program--score mapping grows more complex, limiting their practical utility. We argue that explicit gradients are not essential for effective program optimization. Leveraging their prior knowledge, LLMs can propose plausible atomic edits directly from the current program, thereby enabling a zeroth-order optimization strategy. However, zeroth-order search suffers from a \textit{weakest-link effect}: when a bundle of edits is accepted or rejected as a whole, a single harmful edit can negate the benefits of all remaining edits. To address this issue, we introduce HERO, a program optimizer that prompts an LLM to generate diverse, non-overlapping atomic edits and then systematically selects and composes them into coherent program improvements using evaluator scores. We evaluate HERO across algorithmic problems, strategy games, the design of LLM-based agentic systems, and robotic path planning. Across these domains, HERO consistently discovers higher-scoring programs and converges substantially faster than prior LLM-based optimizers, while consuming fewer tokens.

</details>


### [156] [Mining Verdict Boundaries for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28954)
*Jiawei Ren,Guanqin Zhang,Zhenya Zhang,Yulei Sui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分支定界（BaB）验证神经网络时需沿树路径逐节点求解、导致边界传播昂贵的效率问题，提出利用路径单调性同时分裂多个激活函数（如ReLU）进行指数搜索，快速定位判定边界，并利用子问题求解的定量信息增强搜索，显著减少边界无关子问题的处理，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BaB方法在搜索已验证与未验证子问题的判定边界时，沿树路径逐层深入，需在每个节点（子问题）进行代价高昂的边界传播，效率低下。亟需更高效的边界搜索策略来减少不必要的子问题求解。

Method: 提出利用每条路径的单调性，同时分裂多个激活函数（如ReLU）而非逐个处理，进行指数搜索以高效定位判定边界；增强版本进一步利用子问题求解获得的定量信息估计边界位置，从而跳过大量与边界无关的子问题。

Result: 在常用基准测试上的实验表明，所提技术能够有效加速BaB验证过程，与近期基于BaB的方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过指数搜索和边界位置估计，可大幅提升BaB验证神经网络的效率，避免逐节点传播的高昂代价，为完整验证提供了更实用的路径。

Abstract: Branch and Bound (BaB) aims to achieve complete verification of neural networks by adaptively partitioning the problem and applying off-the-shelf verifiers to subproblems. Its problem-splitting history can be represented as a tree, where each subproblem corresponds to a child node. A key problem of BaB lies in searching for the verdict boundaries across all the paths that divide the verified and unverified subproblems. We observe that the existing BaB approach tackles this problem by solving each expensive subproblem sequentially along the tree path as its depth increases, requiring costly bounds propagation at every visited BaB tree node (i.e., subproblem), which is inefficient. To address this issue, we propose effective search approaches that leverage the monotonicity of each path to efficiently and precisely locate the verdict boundary by simultaneously splitting multiple activation functions (e.g., ReLU), rather than processing them one at a time as in the classical approach. Our approach performs an effective exponential search along each path, allowing us to skip many boundary-unrelated subproblems when identifying the verdict boundary. The enhanced version further improves this process by estimating the boundary's position using quantitative information obtained from subproblem solving. We perform experimental evaluation on commonly-used benchmarks to assess our proposed techniques, and compare them with recent BaB-based approaches.

</details>


### [157] [Efficient LLM Adversarial Training via Low-Rank Defense and Circuit-Guided Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28959)
*Weiyi He,Yuping Lin,Jiliang Tang,Yue Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大型语言模型（LLM）的对抗训练计算成本过高的问题，从防御侧和攻击侧两个角度提出加速潜在对抗训练（LAT）的计算高效策略。防御侧采用表征微调（ReFT）并揭示令牌匹配问题；攻击侧通过提取相关电路构建轻量级替代模型来生成对抗攻击。理论分析和实验表明，与标准LAT全微调相比，该方法平均每步对抗训练FLOPs减少48.1%，且仅需0.0118%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是对抗攻击最有效的防御之一，但在现代规模下计算成本极高，尤其是大型语言模型。现有的加速策略如潜在对抗训练（LAT）仍然计算开销大，亟需更高效的方法。

Method: 从两个互补视角出发：(1) 防御侧优化：在LAT中探索表征微调（ReFT），并指出ReFT与攻击之间可能存在令牌应用不匹配的问题。(2) 攻击侧优化：在每次LAT迭代计算对抗攻击时，仅提取LLM中的相关电路构建轻量级替代模型，避免在全模型上进行前向-反向传播计算。两种策略均提供理论证明和数值验证。

Result: 与标准LAT全微调相比，该方法平均每步对抗训练的FLOPs减少48.1%，且仅需0.0118%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: 所提出的双视角优化策略能显著降低对抗训练的计算成本，同时保持防御有效性，为LLM的高效对抗训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Adversarial training is one of the most effective defenses against adversarial attacks, yet the computational cost remains prohibitive at modern scales, especially for large language models (LLMs). While existing mitigation strategies, e.g., latent adversarial training (LAT), have been developed, they still incur a high computational cost. In this work, we comprehensively investigate computation-efficient strategies to speed up LAT from two complementary perspectives: (1) Defense-side optimization: We explore the representation fine-tuning (ReFT) within LAT, and reveal a potential issue if there is a mismatch on which tokens to apply ReFT and the attack. (2) Attack-side optimization: When computing adversarial attacks in each LAT iteration, we extract only the relevant circuits from the LLM to construct a lightweight surrogate model, avoiding the computation in the forward-backward passes through the full model during the attack generation. For both perspectives, we provide theoretical justifications and numerical evidence to illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed strategies. Ultimately, compared to standard LAT with full fine-tuning, our method on average reduces per-step adversarial-training FLOPs by 48.1% while requiring only 0.0118% trainable parameters.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Feature and Structure Alignment: Learning Transferable Propagation Knowledge for Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28980)
*Yi Wang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种传播感知的图基础模型（ProGFM），将边与特征维度之间的传播关系视为可迁移的知识单元，通过学习跨域传播知识实现自适应信息聚合，在跨域图迁移任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型主要关注通过特征对齐和结构对齐来缓解域差异，但忽略了图数据中可迁移知识单元的探索；同时，它们通常依赖固定的传播机制，忽视了不同边在不同特征维度上可能具有不同传播模式的异质性。

Method: ProGFM将边与特征维度之间的传播关系作为可迁移的知识单元，通过构建传播关系原型库来学习跨域可迁移的传播知识，从而在未见过的图域中实现自适应的信息聚合。

Result: 在多种跨域迁移场景下的实验表明，ProGFM具有较强的跨域知识迁移能力，相比现有方法展现出更优的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模传播关系作为可迁移知识单元，ProGFM有效解决了图数据缺乏统一表示单元的问题，为构建更强大的图基础模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling knowledge transfer across diverse domains. Unlike traditional graph learning methods that are typically designed for in-domain settings, GFMs aim to learn transferable knowledge that can generalize to unseen graph domains. However, unlike language or visual data, graphs lack intrinsic and unified representation units, such as tokens in language and patches in vision, making it challenging to identify transferable knowledge units for building graph foundation models. Existing graph foundation models mainly focus on mitigating domain discrepancies through feature alignment and structure alignment, while overlooking the exploration of transferable knowledge units underlying graph data. Moreover, these methods generally rely on fixed propagation mechanisms during message passing, overlooking the heterogeneity in propagation patterns, as different edges may exhibit distinct propagation patterns for different feature dimensions. To address these limitations, we propose a Propagation-aware Graph Foundation Model (ProGFM), which regards the propagation relationships between edges and feature dimensions as transferable knowledge units. Through a propagation relationship prototype bank, ProGFM learns cross-domain transferable propagation knowledge, enabling adaptive information aggregation in unseen graph domains. Extensive experiments across various cross-domain transfer scenarios demonstrate that ProGFM possesses strong cross-domain knowledge transfer capability and exhibits superior generalization performance compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [159] [SILVA Networks as Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28989)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SILVA Networks 是一种结构化隐式神经网络架构，通过将刺激、局部交互、全局交互、阻尼和读出等组件显式分离到一个不动点框架中，使得隐式层的内部交互动态可训练、可消融、可可视化和可诊断，并在图像、分子、引文网络和长程图基准上展示了任务依赖性的各组件作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经层将直接输入、局部结构、全局上下文等不同来源的信息都吸收到单一的不动点更新中，难以区分哪些来自刺激、哪些来自局部传播、哪些来自全局上下文、哪些来自求解器动力学。因此需要一种能够解耦这些不同影响因素的隐式表示架构。

Method: 提出 SILVA (Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields) 网络，在不动点架构内显式分离了五个组件：刺激（stimulus）、局部交互（local interaction）、全局交互（global interaction）、阻尼（damping）和读出（readout）。通过领域特定的节点、邻域和全局摘要定义，将同一模板实例化到图像、分子、引文网络和长程图基准等不同任务上。

Result: 实验和消融研究显示了各组件任务依赖性的角色：局部交互在图任务中起重要作用；MNIST 在测试容量下从循环中获得的好处很小；最明显的全局收益出现在长程节点分类基准上。SILVA 提供了可训练、可消融、可可视化和可诊断的内部交互动态。

Conclusion: SILVA 网络通过显式分离隐式层中的不同交互来源，不仅提高了模型的可解释性，还揭示了不同任务中局部、全局和刺激信息的差异化贡献，为设计更透明、可诊断的隐式神经网络提供了新方向。

Abstract: Many learning problems require representations that reconcile direct input, nearby structure, and broader context. In implicit neural layers, these influences are usually absorbed into a single fixed-point update, making it hard to identify what enters from the stimulus, what propagates locally, what comes from global context, and what is produced by solver dynamics. Here we introduce SILVA Networks, Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors via Dynamic Interaction Fields. SILVA separates stimulus, local interaction, global interaction, damping, and readout inside one fixed-point architecture. The same template is instantiated for images, molecules, citation networks, and long-range graph benchmarks through domain-specific definitions of nodes, neighborhoods, and global summaries. Experiments and ablations show task-dependent roles for these terms: local interactions are load-bearing in the graph tasks, MNIST gains little from recurrence at the tested capacity, and the clearest global benefit appears in a long-range node-classification benchmark. SILVA therefore provides an implicit representation whose internal interaction dynamics can be trained, ablated, visualized, and diagnosed.

</details>


### [160] [Dynamics-aware identification of governing equations from sparse and noisy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29036)
*Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Koopman算子理论（基于DMD、EDMD和优化DMD）的上采样技术，对稀疏和噪声时间序列数据进行插值和去噪，以提高SINDy和PDE-FIND方法在导数估计和稀疏回归中的鲁棒性。在Lorenz-63、Van der Pol ODE系统以及Burgers、Fisher-KPP、线性对流-扩散PDE系统上的实验表明，多项式EDMD在ODE系数恢复上表现最佳，而低秩DMD辅助重构在Burgers和对流-扩散PDE中有效，但Fisher-KPP中原始基线仍具竞争力。与线性插值和样条插值相比，Koopman预处理整体上提升性能，并能稳定Pareto支持集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏和噪声的时间测量数据会导致导数估计不可靠，从而影响SINDy和PDE-FIND等稀疏辨识方法从数据中恢复控制方程的能力。因此需要一种动力学感知的预处理方法来改善导数估计误差。

Method: 采用基于Koopman算子的上采样技术，包括动态模态分解（DMD）、扩展DMD（EDMD）和优化DMD。这些方法学习选定可观测量上的有限维Koopman演化近似，用于在观测时间窗口内对快照进行插值和去噪，然后再进行导数估计和稀疏回归。

Result: 多项式EDMD在ODE系统上表现最强，尤其在系数精度方面。PDE结果具有系统依赖性：低秩DMD辅助重构改进了Burgers和对流-扩散PDE的发现，而Fisher-KPP数据中原始基线（无上采样）仍具竞争力。Koopman预处理整体优于线性插值和样条插值，并且DMD辅助上采样能稳定Pareto非oracle支持集选择。

Conclusion: Koopman上采样作为一种动力学感知的预处理步骤，当其可观测量表示和低秩结构适合数据时，能够有效减少导数估计误差。该方法应根据具体系统特性选择使用，并非在所有情况下都优于原始基线。

Abstract: Sparse identification of nonlinear dynamics (SINDy) and PDE functional identification (PDE-FIND) recover parsimonious ordinary and partial differential equations (ODEs and PDEs) from data. However, sparse and noisy temporal measurements can make derivative estimates unreliable. To address this problem, we evaluate Koopman-based upsampling techniques implemented with dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), extended DMD (EDMD), and optimized DMD. These methods learn finite-dimensional approximations of Koopman evolution on selected observables and are used to interpolate and denoise snapshots inside the observed time window before derivative estimation and sparse regression. The empirical benchmark comprises two ODE systems, Lorenz-63 and Van der Pol, and three periodic PDE systems, Burgers, Fisher-Kolmogorov-Petrovskii-Piskunov (Fisher-KPP), and linear advection-diffusion, over sparse and noisy sampling regimes. Polynomial EDMD gives the strongest ODE results, especially in coefficient accuracy. The PDE results are system-dependent: low-rank DMD-assisted reconstructions improve Burgers and advection-diffusion discovery, while the raw baseline (without upsampling) remains competitive for the Fisher-KPP data. A comparison against linear and smoothing-spline interpolation techniques shows that the selected Koopman-based preprocessors provide overall performance gains over these non-dynamical alternatives. We also demonstrate that DMD-assisted upsampling can stabilize Pareto-based non-oracle support-size selection. Overall, Koopman-based upsampling is best viewed as a dynamics-aware preprocessing step that can reduce derivative-estimation error when its observable representation and low-rank structure are appropriate for the data.

</details>


### [161] [DFSC: Error-Controlled Differentiable Mittag-Leffler Propagation for Fractional Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29038)
*Ning Hu,Haitao Duan,Shuqun Li,Chuyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DFSC 是一个基于 PyTorch 的分数阶科学机器学习环境，核心是 Mittag-Leffler 谱层（MLSL），将已知的分数阶传播与数据驱动校正分离，支持自适应截断、Lanczos 基复用、密集/稀疏/无矩阵等多种算子路径，并提供认证级误差界（中位效应比 1.246）和 GPU 加速，适用于具有明确分数阶结构的正向/逆向问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶科学机器学习需要可微分、可批处理、可加速并与神经网络组合的数值算子。当主导的线性分数阶演化通过 Mittag-Leffler 传播子已知时，反复用历史求解器重建或从数据重新学习该响应是低效的。因此需要一种将已知分数阶传播与数据驱动校正分离的框架，避免不必要的计算冗余。

Method: 提出 DFSC 环境，核心是 Mittag-Leffler 谱层（MLSL），该层将已知的分数阶传播与数据驱动校正分离，使得神经网络模块仅学习未解决的动力学，同时联合优化分数阶和残差网络参数。采用自适应算法，根据逐次可微评估是否满足给定容差来增加特殊函数截断深度或 Lanczos 维度。在负实交替级数区域提供认证的首项省略误差界，其余区域明确标记为经验估计。支持密集、稀疏、无矩阵、自伴、广义和受控复算子路径，可训练分数阶，直接反问题，残差神经组合，CPU/GPU 执行。

Result: 认证级数界限覆盖全部 59 个合格参考案例，中位界限/误差效应比为 1.246（已解析误差）。复用预制的批处理 Lanczos 基可保持相同的固定路径值，并将重复查询时间在 CPU 上减少 4.61–7.11 倍，在 RTX 5070 上减少 13.07–16.22 倍（不包括一次性准备）。27 个案例的逆矩阵在整体上发现满秩局部曲率，同时保持显式的模型条件性。

Conclusion: DFSC 作为一个具有误差感知的可选基元，适用于具有匹配分数阶结构的问题，而非作为分数阶求解器或神经模型的通用替代品。结合外部求解器和混合真实数据的结果支持其在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Fractional scientific machine learning requires numerical operators that can be differentiated, batched, accelerated, and composed with neural networks. When the dominant linear fractional evolution is known through a Mittag-Leffler propagator, repeatedly reconstructing that response with a history solver or relearning it from data is unnecessary. We present DFSC, a PyTorch environment organized around the Mittag-Leffler Spectral Layer (MLSL). The layer separates known fractional propagation from data-driven corrections, so neural modules learn only unresolved dynamics while fractional orders and residual-network parameters are optimized jointly. Its adaptive algorithm increases special-function truncation depth or Lanczos dimension until successive differentiable evaluations satisfy a requested tolerance. In the negative-real alternating-series regime, DFSC additionally returns a certified first-omitted-term bound; outside that regime it explicitly labels estimates as empirical.
  DFSC supports dense, sparse, matrix-free, self-adjoint, generalized, and controlled complex operator paths; trainable fractional orders; direct inverse problems; residual neural composition; and CPU/GPU execution. The certified series bound covers all 59 eligible reference cases, with median bound/error effectivity 1.246 for resolved errors. Reusing a prepared batched Lanczos basis gives identical fixed-path values and reduces repeated-query time by 4.61--7.11 times on CPU and 13.07--16.22 times on an RTX 5070, excluding one-time preparation. A 27-case inverse matrix finds full-rank local curvature throughout, while remaining explicitly model-conditional. External solver and mixed real-data results support DFSC as an error-aware optional primitive for matched fractional structure, rather than a general replacement for fractional solvers or neural models.

</details>


### [162] [Learning Lookahead Lemmas for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29051)
*Liam Davis,Haoze Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于“lookahead”过程的神经网络验证内处理框架，通过推导不稳定ReLU阶段的引理并构建蕴涵图来剪枝搜索空间和活化布尔剪枝，显著提升了两款SOTA验证器（Marabou和α-β-CROWN）的性能，最多可多证明34%的不可满足实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络验证器大多采用分支定界（branch-and-bound）作为核心求解机制，但在处理复杂网络时仍面临搜索空间过大、效率不足的问题。本文旨在通过引入“lookahead”内处理技术来增强验证器的推理能力，从而更高效地判定不可满足实例。

Method: 本文提出了一个内处理框架，核心是“lookahead”过程：它在分支定界过程中前瞻性地推导关于不稳定ReLU阶段的新引理，并将这些引理收集到蕴涵图中；利用该蕴涵图对搜索空间进行剪枝，并对布尔剪枝（boolean cuts）进行活化（vivify）。该框架被集成到两个SOTA验证器Marabou和α-β-CROWN中。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，集成该框架的Marabou和α-β-CROWN相比原始版本均获得性能提升，其中不可满足实例的证明数量最多增加了34%。

Conclusion: “lookahead”内处理框架是一种有效的增强手段，能够显著提升分支定界型神经网络验证器的求解能力，未来可进一步推广至更多验证器或与其他技术结合。

Abstract: State-of-the-art neural network verifiers use the branch-and-bound procedure as their core solving mechanism. We introduce an inprocessing framework for neural network verification driven by the lookahead procedure. Under this framework, lookahead derives new lemmas over the phases of unstable ReLUs, which are collected into an implication graph that is used to prune the search space and vivify boolean cuts. We instantiate the framework in two state-of-the-art verifiers, Marabou and $α$-$β$-CROWN, and demonstrate that it improves performance in both, proving up to 34% more instances unsatisfiable.

</details>


### [163] [Who Wins Where? Conformal Model Comparison for Local Superiority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29053)
*Yi Zhou,Baishi Li,Xuan Yao,Ke-Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种基于分裂样本的保形局部模型比较框架，能在不同输入区域自适应地声明局部最优模型，并提供有限样本下的错误控制保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局模型比较会掩盖模型在不同协变量空间区域上的异质性表现，导致无法识别局部最优模型。需要一种能够在局部区域进行校准的模型比较方法。

Method: 提出保形局部模型比较方法，采用三路数据分裂：第一路拟合竞争模型，第二路估计局部均值和尺度（基于样本外得分），第三路进行保形校准以量化残差不确定性。在目标点，仅当单侧保形界排除平局时才声明局部胜者，得分的符号决定偏好模型。

Result: 证明了有限样本下单侧错误声明的边际控制、局部均值估计器在非平局边界处的一致性、全局比较与局部优势流行度可能严重不一致，以及平方损失偏差-方差分解揭示模型结构对局部胜率的影响。合成和真实数据实验表明该方法能恢复异质性胜者区域、在不确定时弃权，并产生比全局选择更高的条件增益。

Conclusion: 保形局部模型比较提供了一种严格且实用的框架，用于在异质性空间中识别局部最优模型，避免了全局比较的误导性结论，并通过保形校准保证了有限样本下的统计可靠性。

Abstract: Standard model comparison is global, aggregating losses across the covariate space to declare a single winner. This can obscure heterogeneous performance, where different models are preferable in different regions. We introduce conformalized local model comparison, a split-sample framework for constructing calibrated local best-model maps. Given a model comparison score, such as the difference between two squared losses, the method uses three disjoint splits to fit competing models, estimate local centers and scales from out-of-sample scores, and conformally calibrate residual uncertainty. At a target point, the procedure declares a local winner only when a one-sided conformal bound excludes a tie, with the score's sign determining the favored model. We prove finite-sample marginal control for one-sided erroneous declarations on the realized future comparison score, establish pointwise consistency of the localized mean-score estimator away from tie boundaries, show that aggregate comparison can disagree sharply with the prevalence of local superiority, and derive a squared-loss bias--variance decomposition that clarifies how model structure affects local wins. Synthetic and real-data experiments show that the method recovers heterogeneous winner regions, abstains under uncertainty, and yields higher conditional gain than global selection.

</details>
