### [195] [Adaptive FastOPD: Progress-Aware Rollout Horizon Expansion for Efficient On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29494)
*Qian Tan,Huaifei Liang,Xuanyu Zhu,Lei Jiang,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Adaptive FastOPD，一种进度感知的自适应策略，根据教师-学生信号相对变化动态扩展 rollout 长度，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低在线蒸馏训练时间（49.1-71.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）计算成本高，主要由于少量长响应延迟批次完成；现有加速方法使用固定预算或绝对阈值，不能反映不同模型和训练阶段的真实学习进度。

Method: 当学习在当前边界区域停滞且当前 horizon 被充分利用时，才扩展 rollout 长度。停滞判断基于四个教师-学生信号相对于进入当前 horizon 时的值的变化，而非固定步长或绝对阈值；同时通过利用率条件防止少量长响应触发不必要的扩展。

Result: 在两个教师-学生对上，Adaptive FastOPD 取得了最高的平均性能，同时训练时间相比 OPD 15K 减少了 49.1-71.2%，且对超参数设置鲁棒。

Conclusion: Adaptive FastOPD 是一种高效的在线蒸馏加速方法，通过进度感知的自适应策略平衡了训练效率与模型性能，具有广泛的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher supervision along student-generated trajectories, but its online rollout process incurs substantial computational cost, particularly when a few long responses delay batch completion. Existing acceleration methods typically control rollout length using fixed budgets or absolute teacher--student agreement thresholds, which may not reflect learning progress across different models and training stages. We propose Adaptive FastOPD, a progress-aware strategy that expands the rollout horizon only when learning near the current boundary region has plateaued and the current horizon is sufficiently utilized. The former is determined from four teacher--student signals measured relative to their values upon entering each horizon, making expansion responsive to stage-specific progress rather than a predefined step interval or an absolute threshold on the raw agreement signals, while the latter prevents a small number of long responses from triggering increases in rollout cost. Across two teacher--student pairs, Adaptive FastOPD achieves the highest average performance while reducing training time by 49.1--71.2\% relative to OPD 15K, and remains robust across a range of hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [196] [Transcript-Managed Transformers: Monotone Multi-Agent Collapse and Universality with Two Pop-Enabled Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29496)
*Sergey Salishev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究固定有限精度因果Transformer的转录管理（transcript management）。定义了一种带k个通道的转录管理换能器（TMT），每个通道可进行push/pop操作，其中无pop的受限版本（RTMT）等价于确定性有限状态换能，而允许pop后，1个通道等价于确定性上下文无关语言（DCFL），≥2个通道则达到递归可枚举（RE）即图灵完备。还证明两个pop使能的通道即可实现通用计算，并讨论了模拟代价与不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解固定精度Transformer在有限上下文下的计算能力边界。通过形式化定义转录管理模型，探究push/pop操作对表达能力的影响，以及这种模型与经典自动机理论（DFA、PDA、TM）的对应关系，从而揭示Transformer在实际应用中可能达到的理论极限。

Method: 定义TMT_n(k)模型：一个有限控制器、k个通道，每轮动作包含stay/push/pop，基于调用者驱动的状态映射。可见窗口编码为有限符号。无pop的RTMT为仅append层。通过将pop使能的通道视为栈，编译到Hopcroft–Ullman表示，建立与经典计算层次的对应。同时考虑多agent编排下的单调协议（append、路由、复制可见块）。

Result: RTMT在固定k下精确实现确定性有限状态换能；相同结论适用于固定有限agent群在单调协议下。引入pop后：k=1时对应DCFL，k≥2时对应RE。两个pop使能的通道（可以分布在1~2个agent中）即可达到通用性。给出了模拟代价，并证明了固定块大小和可见半径下的不变性。

Conclusion: 该研究为固定精度Transformer的转录管理建立了计算能力层级：无pop时等价于有限自动机，1个栈（1 pop通道）等价于下推自动机，≥2个栈达到图灵完备。揭示了上下文窗口受限的Transformer通过stack-like操作可获得强表达能力，但需要多个独立栈才能突破上下文无关语言边界。

Abstract: We study transcript management for fixed, finite-precision causal Transformers. A transcript is partitioned into channels of bounded blocks. Each transition consults a fixed visible suffix and may append one block, leaving the model, weights, and token protocol unchanged. The operation $P_c:=\PopContext(c)$ deletes the newest block on channel $c$ and exposes its predecessor.
  We model the layer by the Transcript-Managed Transducer $\TMTn{k}$: one finite controller, $k$ channels, and per-round actions from stay, push, and pop under a caller-driven status map. Fixed visible windows encode as finite symbols. The pop-free Restricted Transcript-Managed Transducer $\RTMTn{k}$ is the standard append-only layer and, for every fixed $k$, realizes exactly the deterministic finite-state transductions. The same holds for every fixed finite agent population under a monotone protocol that appends, routes, and copies visible blocks.
  Admitting $\{P_c\}_{c=1}^k$ restores pop. Newest-first, a pop-enabled channel is a stack; compiling to the Hopcroft--Ullman presentation transfers the classical hierarchy: $\DCFL$ for $k=1$ and $\RE$ for every $k\ge2$. Orchestrated one-channel agents match one controller with $k$ channels, so two pop-enabled transcripts---in one agent or two---suffice for universality. Simulation costs and invariance to fixed block size and visible radius are stated. The bounds fix precision, alphabets, blocks, visibility, controller state, and population; growing exact context, hidden-block access, writable stores, and unbounded \textbf{Spawn} add further state.

</details>


### [197] [The Grokked Illusion: True Equilibrium Mitigates Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29503)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Lai Shun Chan,Yue Shang,Entao Yang,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，虽然在模块算术的“grokking”现象中，AdamW训练的Transformer和高熵模型（通过Wang-Landau分子动力学采样）都达到了完美泛化，但高熵模型对后续噪声注入（新随机标签数据）具有显著更强的鲁棒性：高熵模型在遗忘原始任务后仍保持约95%的测试准确率，而AdamW模型则遭受灾难性遗忘，准确率降至75%以下。这种“完美泛化但不鲁棒”的现象被称为“grokked illusion”。通过奇异值分解，高熵模型在注意力层和MLP层具有更高的有效秩，表明更丰富的特征表示可作为抵御灾难性遗忘的缓冲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络通常仅通过训练和测试性能来评估，但这些指标无法揭示所学表示的鲁棒性。最近研究表明，参数空间中占据更大体积的解（高玻尔兹曼熵）往往具有更好的泛化能力（高熵优势）。本文进一步探究这种优势是否超越泛化，即模型在后续学习新信息时保持已有知识的能力（鲁棒性）。

Method: 以模块算术中的grokking现象作为受控实验环境，设计噪声注入实验：分别用AdamW训练和Wang-Landau分子动力学（高熵采样）训练两个达到相同饱和性能的Transformer模型，然后强制两者用随机标签完全记住新数据，比较两者在原始任务上的遗忘程度。通过奇异值分解分析网络权重的有效秩。

Result: AdamW训练模型遭受灾难性遗忘，原始任务测试准确率从100%降至75%以下；而高熵模型仍保持约95%的准确率。奇异值分解表明，高熵模型在注意力层和MLP层具有显著更高的有效秩，且这种优势在噪声注入前后均存在。

Conclusion: 完美泛化并不等同于同等鲁棒性。高熵模型的丰富特征表示可作为抵御灾难性遗忘的缓冲，揭示了“grokked illusion”——即表面上看似完美的泛化背后隐藏着脆弱的鲁棒性。这一发现为理解训练模型对干扰的鲁棒性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: While neural networks are typically evaluated by their training and test performance, these metrics do not reveal how robust a learned representation is. Recent studies have shown that solutions occupying larger volumes in parameter space, as quantified by Boltzmann entropy, often exhibit superior generalizability compared to those reached by conventional optimization, a phenomenon known as the high entropy advantage. Here we ask whether this advantage persists beyond generalization. Specifically, we investigate models' robustness, the ability to retain the learned knowledge when the model is subsequently trained to acquire new information. Using grokking in modular arithmetic as a controlled setting, we design a noise injection experiment to evaluate the robustness difference between AdamW-trained transformers and high-entropy model sampled from Wang-Landau Molecular Dynamics with identical saturated performance. By forcing both models to fully remember new data with random labels, we find that AdamW-trained models suffer from catastrophic forgetting, with original task test accuracy dropping from 100% to below 75%, whereas the high-entropy models maintain approximately 95% test accuracy. We term this hidden fragility behind apparent generalization the "grokked illusion." Through singular value decomposition of the neural network weights, we discover that high-entropy neural networks possess significantly higher effective rank in attention and MLP layers both before and after noise injection, indicating richer feature representations can serve as a buffer against catastrophic forgetting. Our findings demonstrate that perfect generalization does not imply equal robustness, offering a new perspective on what makes a trained model robust to interference.

</details>


### [198] [TerraNova: A Foundation Model for the Anthropocene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29527)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraNova 是一个基础模型，首次将物理地球的连续网格数据与社会经济的行政单元数据统一在同一表示空间中。它通过在1024个多模态记录（512网格地球系统场+512国家指标）上训练，使用专用编码器、跨模态transformer和超网络解码器，结合两个对比学习目标（人口加权国家-坐标对齐和地理空间嵌入对齐），实现了既能处理连续空间场又能处理国家层面任务的能力，同时支持不确定性估计、时间动态和快速适应未见变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类世的核心问题之一是建立耦合物理地球与人类社会的统一模型，但现有表示无法同时覆盖两者的观测范围。根本障碍在于几何不匹配：物理地球以连续场形式测量（忽略政治边界），而人类社会按行政单元报告。传统方法需要对有损平均操作来桥接两种几何，导致信息丢失。

Method: TerraNova 采用双编码器架构：位置编码器、国家编码器、时间编码器和任务编码器分别处理不同模态的输入；跨模态transformer将它们融合为共享的时空状态表示。超网络根据查询生成专用解码器，其证据头输出预测分布。两个对比学习目标：人口加权对比损失（将国家表示与其领土内坐标对齐）和地理空间嵌入对比损失（与预训练的地理空间嵌入对齐）。训练数据包含512个网格化地球系统场和512个国家指标，共1024个记录。

Result: TerraNova 的表示能力与专用地理空间编码器相当，同时扩展了它们无法表示的维度（时间、海洋和不确定性），并支持国家层面的能力。冻结的主干网络可以从稀疏观测重建密集场，并在消费级硬件上几分钟内适应未见过的变量。

Conclusion: TerraNova 通过解决物理与社会数据之间的几何不匹配问题，为建立真正耦合的人地系统模型提供了可行框架。其统一表示不仅覆盖了更广泛的数据模态，还具备灵活性、可迁移性和不确定性量化能力，为地球系统与社会经济系统的深度融合建模开辟了新路径。

Abstract: A defining problem of the Anthropocene is to model the physical Earth and human societies as one coupled system, yet no learned representation spans their observational breadth. We argue the obstacle is geometric: the physical Earth is measured as continuous fields that ignore political borders, whereas societies are reported for administrative units. Earth-system foundation models serve the first geometry; coupling it to the second has required lossy averaging over borders. We introduce TerraNova, a foundation model trained on 1,024 physical and societal records in their native geometries: 512 gridded Earth-system fields and 512 national indicators. Dedicated encoders represent location, country, time and task, cross-modal transformers fuse them into a shared spatiotemporal state, and a hypernetwork generates a per-query decoder whose evidential head returns a predictive distribution. Two contrastive objectives couple the representation: a population-weighted alignment between each country and coordinates in its territory, and one to pretrained geospatial embeddings carrying image-derived semantics. Read out through that decoder, the representation is competitive with purpose-built geospatial encoders while spanning axes they do not represent (time, oceans and uncertainty) and supporting country-level capabilities. The frozen backbone reconstructs dense fields from sparse observations and adapts to unseen variables in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [199] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Explainable Early Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29530)
*Ranveer Singh,Pranuthi Tenali,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Vaishali Phatak,Karla Lynch,Daniel Murman,Matthew Rizzo,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化管道，利用预训练基础模型从语言流利性测试的原始音频中提取临床相关变量，构建贝叶斯网络以推理阿尔茨海默病进展标志物及其定性关系，成功恢复了已知临床知识并发现了新的语言标志物关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AD标志物识别依赖人工转录和专家分析，劳动密集且规模受限，亟需自动化的方法来扩展分析规模并发现新的标志物关系。

Method: 使用预训练基础模型处理原始音频，提取临床相关变量，构建贝叶斯网络，利用该网络推理AD进展标志物并推断其定性关系。

Result: 系统成功恢复了已知的临床知识，并识别出语言标志物之间的新关系。

Conclusion: 自动化管道能够有效从音频中提取AD进展相关的定性知识，验证了已知关系并发现了新关联，为大规模AD标志物识别提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Identifying reliable Alzheimer's disease (AD) markers typically requires manual, labor-intensive transcription and expert analysis, limiting its scale. We introduce an automated pipeline that extracts qualitative knowledge about potential AD progression indicators directly from audio recordings of verbal fluency tests. Our method uses pretrained foundation models to process raw audio and extract clinically relevant variables to construct a Bayesian Network (BN); this BN is used to reason about the AD progression markers and infer their qualitative relationships. Our system successfully recovers known clinical knowledge and identifies novel relationships between linguistic markers.

</details>


### [200] [Pyramidal Width Can Increase Under Vertex Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29555)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出了一个反例，证明了Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi在2015年提出的猜想——在添加一个顶点且所有旧顶点仍为顶点的条件下，多面体的金字塔宽度不会增加——是错误的。反例由六个整数点构成的三维多面体P和Q（P有5个顶点，Q在P基础上增加一个顶点）组成，计算表明金字塔宽度平方从48/353增加到36/133，增长因子约为1.4109。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并推翻Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi关于金字塔宽度在顶点添加下单调不减的猜想，通过构造具体反例揭示该猜想的错误。

Method: 构造具有整数顶点的三维多面体P和Q，利用金字塔宽度与面距离的等价性进行转换，通过整数支撑超平面验证面格，并采用有限有理计算精确计算每个面距离，最后附上无依赖的精确验证程序。

Result: 成功构造出反例：P的五个顶点在Q中仍然是顶点，但金字塔宽度平方从48/353增大到36/133，增长因子为√(1059/532)≈1.410886779，直接否定了原猜想。

Conclusion: 原猜想不成立；金字塔宽度在顶点添加时可能增加，即使所有旧顶点都保留为顶点。该反例通过精确有理计算和形式化验证确保了正确性。

Abstract: Lacoste-Julien and Jaggi conjectured in 2015 that the pyramidal width of a polytope cannot increase when a vertex is added, provided that every old point remains a vertex. We give an exact counterexample with six integer points in $\R^3$. For \[ P=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_4\},\qquad Q=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_5\}, \] where \[ \begin{aligned} v_0&=(-1,-3,-1), & v_1&=(3,2,-2), & v_2&=(0,2,1),\\ v_3&=(-1,-3,3), & v_4&=(-2,0,1), & v_5&=(-1,0,-2), \end{aligned} \] all five vertices of $P$ remain vertices of $Q$, but \[ \PWidth(P)^2=\frac{48}{353} \quad\text{and}\quad \PWidth(Q)^2=\frac{36}{133}. \] Thus vertex insertion increases pyramidal width by the factor $\sqrt{1059/532}\approx 1.410886779$. The proof uses the equivalence between pyramidal width and facial distance, certifies both face lattices by integer supporting hyperplanes, and evaluates every facial distance by a finite rational calculation. A dependency-free exact verifier accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [201] [MOT-SR: Multi-Objective Tool-Augmented Scientific Equation Discovery with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29561)
*Boxiao Wang,Runxiang Wang,Kai Li,Chongming Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多目标工具增强的符号回归框架MOT-SR，结合外部分析工具提取结构先验，通过多目标评估（精度、复杂度、泛化性）和动态帕累托前沿引导方程生成，使用两个协作的LLM模块（元策略生成器和方程生成器）进行闭环优化，在40个标准任务和极端质量比旋进轨道建模中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的符号回归方法缺乏数据依赖关系分析机制，且多采用仅关注拟合误差的单目标评估，忽视了结构复杂性和泛化性，导致模型容易过早收敛到局部最优，限制了方程空间的探索。

Method: 提出MOT-SR框架，集成外部分析工具提取结构先验并指导方程生成；采用多目标评估模块（精度、复杂度、泛化性）维护动态帕累托前沿；使用两个协作的LLM模块：元策略生成器（基于帕累托最优方程选择工具和合成结构优化策略）和方程生成器（生成新候选方程）；系统闭环运行，持续优化策略和方程结构。

Result: 在40个标准任务上，MOT-SR在精度、泛化性和效率上优于现有SR方法。在极端质量比旋进轨道建模中，发现的解释性校正项在留出配置上实现了最低的轨迹级积分误差。

Conclusion: MOT-SR通过多目标优化和工具增强，显著提升了符号回归的方程发现能力，特别是在长期科学动力学建模中表现出可靠性和潜力。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) aims to discover analytical equations from observational data and plays a central role in scientific modeling. While recent Large Language Model (LLM) based approaches show promise, they face two limitations. First, they lack data analysis mechanisms for uncovering variable dependencies, which reduces the efficiency of equation discovery. Second, most methods rely on single-objective evaluation focused solely on fitting error. This neglect of structural complexity and generalization often causes models to converge prematurely to local optima, limiting their ability to explore the broader equation space. We propose Multi-Objective Tool-augmented Symbolic Regression (MOT-SR), a unified framework that integrates external analytical tools to extract structural priors and guide equation generation, while jointly optimizing for accuracy, complexity, and generalization via a multi-objective evaluation module that maintains a dynamic Pareto front. MOT-SR employs two collaborative LLM modules: a Meta Strategy Generator, which selects tools and synthesizes structural optimization strategies based on Pareto-optimal equations, and an Equation Generator, which produces new candidate equations accordingly. The system operates in a closed-loop manner, continuously refining both strategies and equation structures. Across 40 standard tasks, MOT-SR outperforms existing SR methods in accuracy, generalization, and efficiency. We further validate MOT-SR on extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) orbital modeling, an important problem in space-based gravitational-wave astronomy where small local errors can accumulate substantially over long-term evolution. The discovered interpretable correction achieves the lowest trajectory-level integration error on held-out configurations. These results demonstrate the potential of MOT-SR to enable reliable modeling of long-horizon scientific dynamics.

</details>


### [202] [Convergence and Regret of the Policy Gradient for Multi-Armed Bandits in Diffusion Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29593)
*Yanwei Jia,Du Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散环境下多臂老虎机问题的策略梯度更新，使用连续时间强化学习框架和logit参数化，证明了在任意恒定学习率下几乎必然收敛到最优臂，并推导了非渐近遗憾上界 O(log T)。通过构造新的Lyapunov函数改进了现有分析，且该函数同样适用于离散时间策略梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续时间强化学习框架下，策略梯度方法在扩散环境中的多臂老虎机问题上的收敛性和遗憾分析尚不充分，已有分析（如Lattimore 2026a）可进一步改进。本文旨在通过SDE工具和Lyapunov函数提供更透明的分析。

Method: 采用Wang et al. (2020)和Jia & Zhou (2022b)的连续时间强化学习框架，将多臂老虎机问题建模为随机微分方程（SDE）描述的扩散环境。策略使用logit参数化，通过构造新颖的Lyapunov函数分析策略梯度更新的动态，证明几乎必然收敛并导出遗憾界。

Result: 在任意恒定学习率下，策略梯度更新几乎必然收敛到最优臂；当学习率低于某个时不变阈值时，非渐近遗憾上界为O(log T)。该Lyapunov函数分析比Lattimore (2026a)的方法更透明，且同样适用于离散时间策略梯度算法。

Conclusion: 本文展示了SDE工具在策略梯度分析中的有效性，Lyapunov函数方法不仅简化了连续时间情形的证明，还能推广到离散时间算法，为强化学习理论提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: This paper studies the policy gradient update for a multi-arm bandit problem in diffusion environment that is described by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) under the continuous-time reinforcement learning framework by Wang et al. (2020), Jia and Zhou (2022b). With the logit parameterization for the stochastic policy, we show that it converges almost surely to the optimal arm under an arbitrary constant learning rate. Furthermore, we derive the non-asymptotic regret upper bound when the constant learning rate is below a time-invariant threshold; and the regret bound has order $O(\log T)$. We improve the analysis in Lattimore (2026a) for the same SDE by constructing a novel Lyapunov function and demonstrate the transparency of analyzing policy gradient using the tools in SDEs. In addition, the same Lyapunov function is also helpful in analyzing the discrete-time policy gradient algorithm.

</details>


### [203] [The Parts Are Greater Than the Sum: Automated Task Sequencing for Efficient Training of Multi-Policy LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29601)
*Jiajia Tang,Sizhe Yuen,Francisco Gomez Medina,Yali Du,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于优化路径组织的自动多策略PEFT框架，通过任务分组与排序将异构任务分配到独立的QLoRA适配空间中，解耦优化路径以缓解干扰和遗忘，在固定参数预算下实现优于单策略和增大容量方法的性能（TRACE基准44.78）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PEFT使用单一共享LoRA适配异构任务序列时，共享优化空间导致任务间干扰、迁移差和灾难性遗忘；现有方法通过增加参数容量或组合多个适配器提升表达力，但仍依赖共享优化路径，未能从根本上解决路径冲突问题。

Method: 提出自动多策略PEFT架构，在固定参数预算下通过任务分组与任务排序自动组织优化兼容的适配路径，每个路径实现为独立的量化低秩适配器（QLoRA），使异构任务在解耦的适配空间中优化，同时保留兼容任务间的正向迁移。

Result: 在TRACE基准上，从单策略PEFT到多策略PEFT性能持续提升，所提自动多策略框架在相同可训练容量下达到最佳性能44.78。

Conclusion: 优化路径组织比单纯增加适配器容量对异构任务参数高效微调更有效。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) commonly adapts large language models using a single shared Low-Rank Adapter (LoRA). This shared optimization space often suffers from interference when adapting heterogeneous task sequences, leading to poor transfer and catastrophic forgetting. Existing approaches mainly improve adapter expressiveness by increasing parameter capacity or composing multiple adapters, yet they still rely on a shared optimization path. In this paper, we propose an optimization-path organization framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, implemented as an automatic multi-policy PEFT architecture. Specifically, optimization-compatible adaptation paths are automatically organized through task grouping and task sequencing under a fixed parameter budget. The organized optimization paths are implemented as independent Quantized Low-Rank Adapters (QLoRA), enabling heterogeneous tasks to be optimized in decoupled adaptation spaces while preserving positive transfer among compatible tasks. Experiments on the TRACE benchmark demonstrate that performance consistently improves from conventional single-policy PEFT to multi-policy PEFT, with the proposed automatic multi-policy framework achieving the best performance of 44.78 under the same trainable capacity. This suggests that optimization-path organization is more effective than simply increasing adapter capacity for heterogeneous parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [204] [A Human-Centered Validation of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29614)
*Christian Oliva,Luis F. Lago-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EPC分数的模型无关可解释性度量指标，通过平衡特征选择稀疏性与模型性能保持来量化解释质量，并在表格、文本和图像三种模态上验证了其有效性，同时证明高EPC分数与人类判断高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域，深度学习模型的可信可解释人工智能（XAI）需求迫切，但客观评估解释忠实度以及将XAI指标与人类中心理解对齐仍存在关键挑战。

Method: 提出EPC分数（Explainability-Performance Coefficient的扩展），一种模型无关的度量指标，通过显式平衡特征选择稀疏性与保留模型性能之间的权衡来量化解释质量。

Result: 在表格、文本和图像三种模态上的实证验证表明，EPC分数能够有效揭示网络激活、数据维度和解释器性能之间的操作依赖关系；并且高EPC分数与人类词汇情感判断和空间视觉注释高度一致。

Conclusion: EPC分数作为一种模型无关的可解释性评估指标，能够客观量化解释质量并与人类理解对齐，为可信XAI提供了有效的评估工具。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning models in high-risk domains has intensified the need for trustworthy Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). However, objectively evaluating explanation fidelity and aligning XAI metrics with human-centered understanding remain critical open challenges. In this work, we propose a model-agnostic metric, the EPC score, which is an extension of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient (EPC), that quantifies explanation quality by explicitly balancing the trade-off between feature selection sparsity and preserved model performance. Through an empirical validation across tabular, text, and image modalities, we show that the EPC score effectively uncovers operational dependencies among network activations, data dimensionality, and explainer performance. Furthermore, we validate the EPC score against independent human-based explanations, proving that higher EPC scores strongly align with human lexical sentiment judgments and spatial visual annotations.

</details>


### [205] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模仿学习中的两个关键干预措施：专家交互（在线查询）和价值函数估计。主要发现是专家交互可以放松对学习者的表征要求——只需能表达专家的价值函数，而不必直接拟合专家策略。基于此提出在线策略模仿学习算法OVI，在理论上证明了其统计高效性，并给出一个负面结果说明离线算法需要更强的假设。实验表明OVI优于BC、DAgger等基线，尤其在学习者网络表达能力远弱于专家时提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆（BC）存在复合误差和性能瓶颈，尤其当学习者无法完美表征专家策略时（如蒸馏场景）。实践中两种干预（在线专家查询、使用价值函数估计）能提升性能，但缺乏理论理解。本文旨在探究这些干预的本质及其协同作用，特别是专家交互如何降低表征要求。

Method: 提出OVI（Online Value-based Imitation）算法：一种交互式在线策略IL算法，要求学习者能表征专家价值函数，并利用线性最大化预言机（linear maximization oracle）实现计算高效性。同时通过理论分析证明专家交互的必要性：在仅有专家价值函数可实现的假设下，离线算法必须依赖专家策略类的复杂度。

Result: 理论上：专家交互可放松表征要求——只需实现专家价值函数而非策略；交互是必要的，离线算法无法避免策略复杂度依赖。实验上：OVI优于BC、DAgger和离线价值方法，尤其当学习者网络比专家网络表达能力更弱时优势最大。

Conclusion: 专家交互在模仿学习中起到关键作用，它能将表征负担从策略转移到价值函数，从而在模型能力有限时仍能有效学习。OVI算法验证了这一理论洞见，为实际应用（如模型蒸馏）提供了高效且理论基础扎实的替代方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [206] [CENDRe: Concept Extraction with Natural Domain Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29621)
*Antonia Holzapfel,Andres Felipe Posada Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CENDRe is a concept extraction method for time-series CNNs that automatically discovers concepts in both time and frequency domains, localizes them via gradient-based masks, and assigns relevance scores, achieving better importance correctness and revealing frequency-based patterns that time-domain methods miss.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing time-series concept extraction methods only operate in the time domain (overlooking frequency features), require a predefined number of concepts, and produce localizations misaligned with the model's actual focus. This limits interpretability in critical domains like fault diagnosis where spectral patterns are key.

Method: CENDRe first discovers concepts by clustering per-timestep latent representations in two stages, using silhouette-guided aggregation to automatically determine the number of concepts. It then localizes each concept via gradients of a presence score that contrasts latent representations with learned prototypes, producing time-domain masks. These gradients are backpropagated through a differentiable invertible mapping (e.g., Fourier transform) to obtain frequency-domain localizations. Finally, each concept receives a relevance score quantifying its contribution to each class.

Result: On synthetic benchmarks, CENDRe achieves representation correctness comparable to state-of-the-art CE methods and significantly higher importance correctness. On real bearing-fault data, it extracts frequency bands driving model predictions, located in regions commonly used for fault diagnosis, providing interpretability that time-domain CE methods cannot.

Conclusion: CENDRe effectively addresses key limitations of prior time-series concept extraction by enabling automatic concept discovery, dual-domain (time and frequency) localization, and aligned masks, offering more faithful and practically useful explanations for CNN-based time-series classifiers.

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for time-series classification, but their deployment in critical domains requires understanding the temporal and spectral patterns that drive their predictions. Concept extraction (CE) methods identify such patterns by analyzing representations within the models' latent space. However, existing time-series CE methods have three limitations: they operate only in the time domain and overlook frequency features, predefine the number of concepts, and produce localizations misaligned with the regions the model uses. We address these limitations by proposing CENDRe, a concept extraction method for CNNs. It first discovers concepts by clustering per-timestep latent representations in two stages, where silhouette-guided aggregation selects the number of concepts automatically. Then, it localizes each concept through gradients of a presence score that contrasts the latent representations with their prototypes, producing masks that concentrate on the regions driving the concept. These gradients, propagated through a differentiable invertible mapping of the input such as a Fourier transform, yield localizations for the same concepts in the frequency domain. Finally, each concept receives a relevance score that quantifies its contribution to each class. On synthetic benchmarks, CENDRe achieves representation correctness comparable to state-of-the-art CE methods and significantly higher importance correctness. On real bearing-fault data, CENDRe extracts the frequency bands driving the model's predictions, located in regions commonly inspected for fault diagnosis, producing evidence to assess the model that time-domain CE methods cannot.

</details>


### [207] [GQ-FSL: Green Quantized Federated Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29659)
*Idan Roth,Lutz Lampe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes GQ-FSL, a green quantized federated split learning framework that uses stochastic quantization with asymmetric precision levels for client/server submodels to reduce energy consumption while maintaining accuracy, by jointly optimizing the split point and quantization precision under a target accuracy constraint.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deploying state-of-the-art DNNs at the wireless edge is severely bottlenecked by strict energy and resource constraints of mobile devices. Federated split learning (FSL) reduces on-device computation but still incurs significant energy consumption due to continuous exchange of cut-layer data and submodels, motivating a more energy-efficient approach.

Method: GQ-FSL incorporates stochastic quantization for both local collaborative training and wireless transmissions, supporting asymmetric precision levels for client- and server-side submodels. Parameterized energy models for the split architecture are developed, and a theoretical convergence bound under statistically heterogeneous data is derived. A joint optimization problem is formulated to configure the DNN split point and precision levels, minimizing total system energy consumption while satisfying a strict target accuracy constraint.

Result: GQ-FSL enables large-scale DNN deployment on resource-constrained devices, achieving superior energy efficiency compared to quantized federated learning and full-precision FSL, while effectively decoupling device energy constraints from global convergence degradation.

Conclusion: GQ-FSL provides a green and efficient solution for edge learning by optimizing quantization and split strategies, demonstrating that asymmetric precision and joint optimization can significantly reduce energy consumption without sacrificing accuracy.

Abstract: Deploying state-of-the-art deep neural networks (DNNs) at the wireless edge is severely bottlenecked by the strict energy and resource constraints of mobile devices. While federated split learning (FSL) mitigates on-device computation by offloading workloads to an edge server, this may introduce systemic overheads, while the continuous exchange of cut-layer data, and submodels still incurs significant energy consumption (EC). To address this, we propose a green quantized FSL (GQ-FSL) framework that incorporates stochastic quantization for both local collaborative training and wireless transmissions. Notably, GQ-FSL supports asymmetric precision levels for the client- and server-side submodels, effectively decoupling device energy constraints from global convergence degradation. To quantify these tradeoffs, we develop parameterized energy models for the split architecture and derive a theoretical convergence bound under statistically heterogeneous data. Building on that, we formulate a joint optimization problem to configure the DNN split point and precision levels, minimizing the total system EC while satisfying a strict target accuracy constraint. Ultimately, we demonstrate that GQ-FSL enables large-scale DNN deployment on resource-constrained devices, achieving superior energy efficiency compared to quantized federated learning and full-precision FSL.

</details>


### [208] [Freeze, Then Select: Structured Field Adapters and Stability-Validated Weak Selection for PDE Discovery from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29665)
*Juncheng Zhong,Chenghuang Shen,Jianfeng Liu,Zhengdong Xiao,Longjiu Luo,Qianrong Wang,Wenjun Xu,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A freeze-then-select method for PDE discovery from sparse observations: a structured field adapter factorizes the field into spatial features and temporal spline coefficients, then Stability-Validated Weak Selection (SVWS) identifies recurrent terms across weak-form systems. Achieves state-of-the-art support recovery on sparse MDBench benchmarks, including challenging Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE discovery from sparse observations requires simultaneous field reconstruction and term selection. Coupled neural PDE discovery methods exhibit three inconsistent support behaviors (persistent, transient, or missing), motivating the need to decouple equation selection from neural optimization to improve reliability and accuracy.

Method: First, train a structured field adapter (without PDE residual) that factorizes the observed field into learned spatial features and temporal coefficients represented by cubic splines. After freezing the field representation, apply Stability-Validated Weak Selection (SVWS): identify recurrent terms across independent weak-form systems, refit candidate supports, and select the final equation on held-out weak-form systems. The same principle is applied to genetic programming to recover expressions like power-law nonlinear diffusion.

Result: Across all six sparse MDBench regimes, the method attains the highest exact support recovery rate, with clearest gains over classical and neural baselines on challenging Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics.

Conclusion: Decoupling field reconstruction from equation selection significantly improves PDE discovery from sparse and noisy observations. The freeze-then-select strategy works both with fixed libraries and genetic programming, enabling recovery of unknown functional forms.

Abstract: PDE discovery from sparse observations requires reconstructing a continuous field and selecting the correct differential terms. Our analysis of optimization paths in coupled neural PDE discovery reveals three behaviors: the exact support can persist to the end of training, appear only transiently, or fail to emerge. To decouple equation selection from neural optimization, we develop a freeze-then-select method combining a structured field adapter with Stability-Validated Weak Selection (SVWS). Trained from observations without a PDE residual, the adapter factorizes the field into learned spatial features and temporal coefficients represented by cubic splines. After freezing the field, SVWS identifies recurrent terms across independent weak-form systems, refits candidate supports, and selects the final equation on held-out weak-form systems. Beyond fixed libraries, we apply the same principle to expressions generated by genetic programming and recover the power-law form of an unknown nonlinear diffusion function from sparse, noisy observations. Across all six sparse MDBench regimes, our method attains the highest exact support recovery rate, with its clearest gains over classical and neural baselines on challenging Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层式Agentic AI架构，使用OpenClaw（代理运行时编排）和Ollama（LLM推理层）构建全栈自主AI代理系统，验证了持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非单一模型，并讨论了扩展性、安全性、隐私、治理和评估等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从反应式LLM到持久性、能行动的自主AI代理的快速转型暴露了Agentic AI架构理解的重大空白，特别是在推理、编排和执行层的分离方面。目前缺乏统一的全栈代理系统设计与评估框架。

Method: 提出一种分层架构，将Agentic AI划分为推理层（Ollama提供LLM推理）、编排层（OpenClaw提供代理运行时编排，集成推理、工具使用和动作执行）和执行层。通过原型实验验证OpenClaw-Ollama架构，评估持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力。

Result: 实验表明，持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非独立模型，性能随架构复杂度增加而持续提升。同时识别了可扩展性、安全性、隐私、治理和评估等方面的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究为Agentic AI建立了统一的架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI代理的有效性，并为构建可扩展、安全且可信的代理系统提供了路线图。所有模型、代码和数据集已公开发布以支持可重复性和基准测试。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [210] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个基于自动化同行评审的基准测试协议，利用前沿大语言模型（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度评估AI科学家系统生成的论文。对四个AI科学家框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）和FARS基准系统生成的75篇论文进行评估，发现FARS显著优于其他系统（平均分2.14-2.47 vs 1.00-1.87），Gemini与Claude的评估高度一致（ρ=0.907），而GPT-5.4一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32）。该工作建立了首个AI科学家系统定量基准，并证明多模型LLM评估可提供可扩展、一致的自动评审方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏评估和比较AI生成论文质量的标准化方法，亟需一个严谨、可重复的基准测试协议来衡量自主研究系统的性能。

Method: 设计了一个自动同行评审系统，使用GPT-5.4、Gemini和Claude三个独立LLM评审员，对论文在原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个核心维度进行评分（1-5分制）。在15个一致的研究提案上运行四个AI科学家框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）生成60篇论文，并与15篇FARS基准论文一同评估。

Result: FARS基准论文的平均分（2.14-2.47）显著高于其他系统（1.00-1.87），在Gemini和Claude评估中得分是次优系统的2倍以上。Gemini与Claude评分高度相关（ρ=0.907, p<0.001），两者与综合评分极强相关（ρ=0.961, p<0.001）；但GPT-5.4与其他评审员的一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32），表明其采用不同评价标准。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个AI科学家系统的定量基准，验证了多模型LLM评估作为可扩展、一致的自动评审框架的可靠性，为自主研究质量的评估提供了新方法。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [211] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段流水线（区域搜索、反思验证、形式化验证）用于自动发现具有高"问题品味"的重大数学猜想，并在 Lean 4 中完成形式化验证，实验表明 20 个候选猜想全部通过解析、类型检查且未被现有自动化工具直接解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，缺乏统一的系统化生成与验证方法，因此需要一种能够自动发现具有真正数学潜力（即能重组研究领域语言并持久助力人类研究）的猜想的方法。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 区域搜索：从显式局部证据模块出发搜索候选猜想区域；(2) 反思验证：对候选猜想的基础性、新颖性和潜在意义进行反思性验证；(3) 形式化验证：在 Lean 4 和 Mathlib 中进行形式化验证（包括解析、类型检查、exact? 吸收测试和 aesop 自动证明测试）。

Result: 在 20 个候选猜想上实验，全部通过 Lean 解析和类型检查，20/20 未被 exact? 直接吸收，20/20 未被 aesop 自动证明，且未发现显式重复或近似重复。表明从自然语言到形式化检查的稳定转换。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够系统性地生成和验证具有高"问题品味"的猜想，且候选猜想具有基础性、新颖性和潜在意义，未被现有自动化定理证明工具轻易解决，为重大猜想的自动发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [212] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkReset 通过显式构造可复用的中间接口（interface writeback and reset）并直接优化重置后的持续推理，解决了长链思维在有限上下文窗口下的冗余积累、上下文溢出和错误锚定问题，从而在多个长程推理基准上一致提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维（long chain-of-thought）在提升复杂问题性能的同时，引入了冗余积累、上下文溢出和错误锚定。在有限上下文窗口下，核心瓶颈并非轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史并支持持续求解。此外，结果奖励驱动的长链强化学习在窗口即将耗尽时会导致模型提前猜测而非继续谨慎推理。

Method: 提出 ThinkReset，一种文本空间的实现。它通过接口写回（interface writeback）和重置（reset）显式构造可复用的中间接口，并直接优化重置后的持续推理成功。

Result: 在多个长程推理基准上，ThinkReset 在固定上下文窗口下一致提高了成功率。

Conclusion: 缺乏可复用的中间接口是有限上下文窗口下长链推理的主要瓶颈，ThinkReset 通过显式构造此类接口并优化后续推理有效性，为解决该问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [213] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种任务感知提示重写器（TAPR），通过强化学习（GRPO）和LLM作为评判者的奖励机制，将用户原始提示重写为任务优化提示，以提升下游LLM性能，在问答、摘要、算术推理等任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通常需要精心设计的提示才能发挥最佳性能，这对非专业用户构成了障碍。本文旨在自动将用户提示重写为任务优化提示，降低提示工程门槛，提升下游任务表现。

Method: 使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行强化学习训练TAPR模型；奖励信号来自LLM作为评判者对重写后提示和对应任务输出的联合评估；基础模型为Phi-4-mini-instruct。

Result: 在问答（Natural Questions）、摘要、算术推理（GSM8K）等多个基准上，TAPR生成的提示包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言，相较于基础模型在提示重写能力上取得了一致性提升。

Conclusion: TAPR能有效将用户提示自动重写为任务优化提示，降低提示工程门槛并提升LLM下游任务性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [214] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR 是一个面向跨域序列推荐（CDSR）的生成式推荐框架。它通过跨域混合分词机制（多塔架构、层级共享-特有与细粒度码本）联合捕获跨域共性与领域特异性，并设计串-并行解码策略利用层级语义标识（SID）结构部分并行化生成过程，从而在保持生成一致性的同时大幅降低推理延迟。实验表明，相比 SOTA 基线，GenCDSR 平均准确率提升 1.5%，推理延迟降低 85.1%。代码与数据集已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在跨域场景下存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 分词阶段忽略跨域协同相关性；(2) 生成阶段使用束搜索等低效解码策略，阻碍实时部署。因此需要一种既能捕获跨域协作信号又具备高效解码能力的生成式 CDSR 框架。

Method: GenCDSR 包含两大核心设计：(1) 跨域混合分词机制（Cross-domain Hybrid Tokenization），采用多塔架构与层级共享-特有码本（hierarchical shared-specific codebooks）及细粒度码本，联合建模跨域共性与领域特异性；(2) 跨域串-并行解码策略（Cross-domain Serial-Parallel Decoding），利用层级 SID 结构将生成过程部分并行化，在保证序列一致性的同时显著降低推理延迟。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，GenCDSR 相比 SOTA 基线实现了平均 1.5% 的准确率提升和平均 85.1% 的推理延迟降低。

Conclusion: GenCDSR 有效解决了现有生成式 CDSR 方法忽略跨域协同相关性与解码效率低下的问题，验证了通过精心设计的分词与解码策略可以兼顾准确性与实时性，为生成式推荐在跨域场景下的实际部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [215] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个生产级抽取层，将实时文档流转换为与正式本体对齐的验证知识图谱，采用本体引导的两遍Qwen3.5-9B抽取和五阶段精炼管道，将搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%且无错误合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型抽取实体和关系时存在不一致：类型词汇碎裂、人名变体、关系重复、同名混淆，亟需一个可靠的生产级系统将非结构化文档转化为结构化知识图谱。

Method: 系统从Kafka消费文档元数据，按格式路由到对应处理器，使用本地Qwen3.5-9B模型（基于本体微调）进行两遍抽取。核心创新是本体引导抽取：实时从图数据库通过嵌入相似度检索本体相关片段注入提示词，将目录开销降低约94%。之后经过五阶段精炼：确定性清洗、跨块合并、第二遍关系抽取、六种无推理去重算法、以及任何相似度分数无法覆盖的嵌入冲突解决子系统。

Result: 在情报语料上搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%，无错误合并，修正了七类静默质量缺陷（如单字符截断、带标题前缀实体的系统重复）。

Conclusion: 验证了本体引导的抽取结合多阶段无模型精炼管道，可以在生产环境中实现高质量、一致性的知识图谱构建，大幅提升检索效果并消除LLM固有的抽取不一致问题。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [216] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SARE（Step-Aware Reasoning Energy）框架，利用相邻 Transformer 层的 Gram 矩阵中心核对齐（CKA）量化每个链式思维推理步骤的几何复杂度，发现推理能量在不同步骤类型中非均匀分布，错误轨迹在关键推理节点表现出系统性较低的能量，且基于 SARE 的特征在多数设置中匹配或超越基于输出的置信度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法依赖输出级信号或将处理深度压缩为单一轨迹级标量，无法揭示推理过程中每一步的计算努力分配情况，因此需要一种能在单个 CoT 步骤粒度上量化推理努力的框架。

Method: SARE 通过计算相邻 Transformer 层间 token 隐藏状态的 Gram 矩阵的 Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) 来量化每一步的推理能量，捕捉跨层的 token 间关系结构，无需特征向量对齐或聚类对应。同时将 CoT 轨迹建模为潜在语义状态之间的转换以关联推理的语义进程。

Result: 在六个推理基准和三个开源 LLM 上，SARE 显示：(1) 推理能量在不同步骤类型上高度非均匀，存在轨迹级指标无法捕获的阶段性转变；(2) 错误轨迹在关键推理节点表现出系统性更低的能量；(3) SARE 特征在多数设定中匹配或超越基于输出的置信度基线。

Conclusion: 内部几何动力学编码了超越表面信号的预测信息，SARE 提供了一种细粒度、无需监督的推理努力量化方法，有助于理解 LLM 推理过程的内部机制。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [217] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文警示：尽管LLM已能通过医学考试甚至在某些场景媲美医生，但其用于患者自主分诊（极少或没有临床医生参与）时缺乏安全证据。核心问题在于LLM优化目标是生成最可能的文本，而非在不对称成本下做出安全决策——漏诊罕见但致命的疾病代价远高于误报。LLM在信息收集不足时无法展现安全分诊所需行为（如拓宽鉴别诊断、主动询问危险信号、降低升级门槛、推迟判断、对未排除的高危诊断保持警惕），而现有评估常使用完整、精心策划的模拟，难以检测这些失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学领域的应用日益广泛，特别是用于症状评估和临床决策支持。其中最危险的场景是让LLM自主对未分诊患者进行分诊而几乎没有临床医生参与。作者感到有必要警示，这种应用目前缺乏安全证据。

Method: 本文为观点性文章（Perspective），通过逻辑论证分析LLM用于自主分诊的根本缺陷：LLM本质上是文本续写模型，优化目标是最可能的文本，而非安全决策；安全分诊需要在不对称成本下进行序贯决策（漏诊代价远高于误报），需要主动信息收集、拓宽鉴别诊断、对低概率但高危害的疾病保持警惕等行为，而这些恰恰是LLM不擅长的。

Result: 指出LLM自主分诊的安全鸿沟不在医学知识，而在临床评估的保真度。LLM可能表现出可信性、顺从性、校准不良等助手式行为，在缺乏临床分诊逻辑约束时被放大。现有评估使用完整、精心策划、置信度门控的模拟，难以检测这些失败模式。

Conclusion: LLM用于完全自主的患者分诊缺乏安全证据，其核心缺陷是信息收集能力不足，无法在不确定性下做出安全的序贯决策。在获得充分验证之前，不应将LLM用于无人监督的临床分诊。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [218] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViSAGE是一个多模态代理记忆框架，通过跨模态绑定、双向记忆精炼和多代理交叉验证，构建自纠正的、以实体为中心的记忆，以解决长时域环境中的实体混淆和幻觉问题，相比最强基线准确率提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理记忆方法在长时域多模态环境中存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 过度压缩和分段处理丢弃了细粒度的身份线索；(2) 严重依赖向量相似性检索，可能返回语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，导致实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉答案。需要一种能保持实体身份一致性、支持时间推理的记忆框架。

Method: ViSAGE包含三个核心机制：(1) 跨模态绑定（Cross-modal Binding）——在长时间范围内锚定实体身份；(2) 双向记忆精炼（Bidirectional Memory Refinement）——传播延迟的身份证据，回溯性统一历史记录并改进未来推理；(3) 多代理交叉验证（Multi-agent Cross-verification）——在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索到的证据，当证据缺失时选择弃权而非给出无支持的答案。

Result: 大量实验表明，ViSAGE在所有评估指标上持续优于最强基线，准确率提升5.9%。

Conclusion: 通过构建自纠正的、以实体为中心的多模态记忆，ViSAGE有效解决了长时域代理中的实体混淆和幻觉问题，验证了跨模态身份绑定、双向精炼和多代理验证机制的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [219] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体路径规划问题，提出了一种基于时空逻辑图算子（STL-GO）的规划方法，并给出了混合整数规划（MIP）和可满足性模理论（SMT）两种编码方案，在多无人机搜救基准测试上验证了其表达能力和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题中存在时空约束（何时何地做什么）和拓扑约束（智能体间如何交互），而现有的形式化框架难以同时有效处理这些约束。STL-GO虽然能够支持多智能体推理及其拓扑关系，但如何将STL-GO规范编码为可求解的规划问题仍是一个挑战，尤其是处理多个随时间变化的图。

Method: 本文提出了两种编码方法：基于混合整数规划（MIP）和基于可满足性模理论（SMT），将STL-GO规范中的图算子（传感、通信、任务拓扑等）编码为规划问题。同时提供了一个统一接口，用于指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规范，使得两种方法可无缝使用和直接比较。

Result: 在多无人机搜救基准测试上进行了实验，通过改变团队规模和图复杂度，展示了所提编码在动态多图交互下的表达能力，并对比了MIP和SMT两种编码的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MIP和SMT编码能够有效求解带有STL-GO约束的多智能体路径规划问题，统一接口便于方法比较，实验验证了编码在复杂拓扑和动态图交互下的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [220] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有科学方程发现基准测试多包含公开已知方程，难以区分模型是真正发现定律还是记忆训练语料。LSR-Synth通过向已知科学机制引入新颖合成项来缓解此问题。本文发现：在现有任务快照下，固定符号词汇表已能覆盖大多数任务，而语言模型生成的候选解很少能扩展可解实例集；仅在词汇覆盖率被选择性破坏时，语言模型的贡献才变得显著。严格分布外评估降低所有方法的绝对成功率，但不改变这一关系。当前任务适合评估表达式拟合与重组能力，但不足以单独识别先验知识对固定搜索空间之外的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试中的方程多为公开已知方程，使评估时难以判断模型是真正从数据中发现定律还是仅仅从训练语料中回忆答案。LSR-Synth通过引入新颖合成项来缓解记忆问题，但本文进一步探究一个更细微的测量问题：这些任务能否有效区分语言模型提供的科学先验知识与不访问任务语义的常规算子搜索？

Method: 构建了一个无语义的固定词汇基线（具有公开可追溯的来源），通过语义盲化、库削弱和匹配算子族剔除等操作评估候选覆盖率的影响。对比语言模型生成的候选解与固定词汇基线的表现，并在严格分布外（OOD）条件下重新评估。

Result: 固定词汇表已覆盖大多数任务；语言模型候选解极少扩展可解实例集；仅在词汇覆盖率被选择性地破坏时，语言模型的边际贡献才变得显著；严格OOD评估降低所有方法的绝对成功率但不改变此关系。

Conclusion: 当前LSR-Synth任务适合评估模型对未见表达式的拟合与重组能力，但不足以单独识别先验知识对固定搜索空间之外的贡献。这并不否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的控制措施，也不意味语言模型先验通常无帮助，而是指出当前任务设计在区分先验贡献方面的局限性。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [221] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对四个智能体安全基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）的验证研究表明，这些基准存在度量缺陷（如恒正策略在F1上获得高分）、小样本导致的排名不一致，以及与能力指标的相关性不稳定。安全声明必须明确指定基准、度量、目标行为和模型面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体安全基准被混用为智能体的整体安全性，但它们测量的行为不同，且其有效性未经系统验证。作者旨在系统评估这些基准的度量特性、排名一致性以及它们与能力指标的关系。

Method: 在官方实现和作者提供的评分器下，对四个基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）在最多22个模型上运行，同时在同一协议下测量MMLU和GPQA作为能力复合指标。分析了度量问题（F1的恒正策略得分）、基准间的排名一致性、相关性随样本量的变化，以及能力与安全指标的相关性。

Result: 恒正策略在R-Judge上达到F1=0.690，超过21个模型中5个有判别能力的模型。三个广覆盖基准对18个模型的排名不一致，且不一致源于小样本伪影（如R-Judge特异性与AgentHarm安全性的相关性从n=7时的-0.64变为n=18时的+0.02）。能力预测任务成功（ρ=+0.60）但与错位安全性负相关（ρ=-0.44，n=21），但在更大面板（n=41）中相关性减弱。AgentHarm与三模板越狱安全性在控制能力后呈强相关（ρ=+0.72），但这属于趋同效度而非通用安全性。

Conclusion: 智能体安全基准的有效性有限，且依赖于具体的选择（基准、度量、目标行为、模型面板）。安全声明必须明确这些要素，否则结论不可泛化。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem. On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [222] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SciToolAgent-Evo，一种面向开放科学工作流、具备本体感知能力的自进化智能体，能够动态获取新工具并完成本体在线集成。同时构建了包含 900 个多难度任务的 OpenSciToolBench 基准，实验表明该方法在鲁棒性和泛化性上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体依赖预定义且语义静态的工具空间，无法适应开放科学工作流中工具需求、能力与边界动态演化的场景。

Method: 提出 SciToolAgent-Evo，由技能、经验与本体化工具图构成的进化记忆驱动；在积累阶段从对比轨迹中蒸馏可泛化知识，在推理阶段通过主动请求与 LinUCB 基 bandit 门控在探索与利用间动态平衡；获取新工具后在线完成其科学本体以无缝集成到已知图中。

Result: 在包含 900 个真实任务、四个难度级别的 OpenSciToolBench 基准上，SciToolAgent-Evo 达到了最先进的性能，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 本体感知与自进化机制能有效提升智能体在动态开放科学工作流中的工具获取与集成能力，从而突破静态预定义工具空间的限制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [223] [EarlyDx: An Admission-Anchored Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation of Evidence-Supported ED-Encounter Diagnoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28788)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,Yutong Guo,Nan Yang,Wenzhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EarlyDx，一个基于MIMIC-IV急诊科就诊记录的大规模开放式早期诊断基准。该基准限制仅使用入院时的可用证据，以急诊科诊断（而非出院诊断）作为监督，并使用LLM审计器验证标签。评估发现，即使是最先进的模型（包括通用大模型、医学专用模型和领域后训练模型）也难以可靠地综合入院时的证据；零样本模型主要依赖提取，仅能恢复3-31%需要推断的诊断；后训练可将推断依赖的召回率提升至56%，但仍有很大差距，在时间关键性条件下没有系统能达到临床医生的灵敏度与精确度平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的诊断预测基准不适合临床入院诊断场景：它们限制预测到封闭的代码集，排除自由文本病历，并使用涵盖整个住院过程的出院诊断作为监督。因此需要一个新的基准来模拟真实的早期诊断场景，即医生在入院时仅凭有限、不完整的证据做出快速诊断。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV中构建了154,834次急诊科就诊的EarlyDx基准。每次就诊仅使用入院时t0可用的记录，并以急诊科记录中的诊断（而非出院诊断）作为监督。使用LLM审计器验证每个自由文本标签是否被证据支持、部分支持或不支持。评估采用语义LLM-as-judge协议，主要评估指标仅计算完全支持的标签。

Result: 零样本模型主要通过提取信息来评分，仅能恢复3-31%需要推断而非直接从记录中读取的诊断。后训练将推断依赖的召回率提升至56%，但仍有显著差距。在时间关键性条件下，没有系统能达到临床医生水平的灵敏度与精确度平衡。

Conclusion: 现有AI系统在早期诊断任务上表现不佳，无法可靠地综合入院时的证据。后训练可以改善但仍有差距，尤其是在时间关键性诊断上。作者发布了完整的构建和评估流程，以推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis at hospital admission must be made rapidly from limited, incomplete evidence. Existing diagnosis-prediction benchmarks are poorly suited to this setting: they restrict prediction to closed code sets, exclude free-text notes, and supervise with discharge diagnoses that incorporate the full inpatient course. We introduce EarlyDx, a large-scale benchmark for open-ended early diagnosis, built from 154,834 emergency department encounters in MIMIC-IV. Each encounter is restricted to records available at admission time $t_0$ and supervised by the diagnoses recorded during the ED encounter rather than at discharge. An LLM auditor further verifies every free-text label as supported, partially supported, or unsupported by that evidence; the primary evaluation scores only fully supported labels. Under a semantic LLM-as-judge protocol, no evaluated system --- frontier general, medical-specialized, or in-domain post-trained --- synthesizes admission-time evidence reliably. Zero-shot models score largely by extraction, recovering only 3-31% of diagnoses that must be inferred rather than read from the record; post-training raises inference-dependent recall to 56%, but a sizeable margin remains, and on time-critical conditions no system attains a clinician's balance of sensitivity and precision. We release the full construction and evaluation pipeline at here.

</details>


### [224] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper introduces an interaction-centric taxonomy for localizing agent failures to specific components (model, harness, environment, grader) and interactions, making failures actionable for repair. The taxonomy organizes 41 failure modes across agent architectures and is evaluated for reproducibility with frontier models achieving Cohen's κ=0.76 against human labels.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing evaluations reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring fault origins and appropriate interventions. This creates a repair-assignment problem where the same visible failure may require different fixes depending on its source. Outcome-level labels and benchmark-specific taxonomies are insufficient for improvement.

Method: The authors propose an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side (model-side, harness-side, environment/grader-side). The taxonomy is grounded in examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged trajectories. Reproducibility is evaluated using independent reasoning agents as judges across four frontier models.

Result: The strongest judge (one of four frontier models) reaches Cohen's κ=0.76 against human category labels, indicating that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences. The taxonomy applies across diverse agent architectures.

Conclusion: The interaction-centric taxonomy provides an actionable structure for diagnosing and repairing agent failures by pinpointing the responsible component and interaction edge, enabling targeted interventions such as model post-training, harness engineering, or environment redesign.

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [225] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了AI伴侣在长期交互中出现的两种故障：人格崩溃（角色、边界、价值观或风格的丧失）和行为漂移（这些属性的逐渐或反复侵蚀）。作者提出了ANCHOR，一种受控合成审计方法，分别测量人格扮演和轨迹回忆。包含2,008段对话，涵盖27种人格、9种交互调度、3种生成记忆设置和4种评估模型。结果显示，没有模型或配置能可靠地保持这两个维度：轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平，且任何上下文条件或记忆都无法一致地解决这些故障。结果表明当前系统尚不能可靠支持长期伴侣连续性，审计必须区分人格扮演、轨迹回忆、评估来源和部署上下文，而非将其合并为单一信任或稳定性评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI伴侣越来越多地调解重复社交互动，用户可能依赖稳定的角色和共享历史，但局部可接受的回复并不能确保这两者得以维持。现有方法无法系统检测长期交互中的连续性问题，论文旨在识别并量化人格崩溃和行为漂移这两种长期故障。

Method: 提出ANCHOR（受控合成审计），分别测量人格扮演（通过密封的102项问卷结合逐轮判断）和轨迹回忆（通过来自35个对话库的110个校准反事实问题评分）。包含2,008段对话，涉及27种人格、9种交互调度、3种生成记忆设置和4种评估模型。

Result: 轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平。没有测试的上下文条件或记忆能一致解决这些故障。问卷保留率因模型和人格侧面而异，与逐轮行为不一致，且对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前系统尚不能可靠支持长期伴侣连续性。审计必须区分人格扮演、轨迹回忆、评估来源和部署上下文，而非将其合并为单一信任或稳定性评分。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [226] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper models the alignment problem where an agent is trained to align with a proxy of human values, and identifies conditions under which the agent's value function becomes catastrophically misaligned (η-catastrophic) as optimization pressure increases. The results show that overoptimization can lead to catastrophic outcomes, motivating AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address the fear that optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values can lead to catastrophic outcomes. The authors aim to formalize this "fragility of human values" in a model of alignment training.

Method: The authors present a model where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that ensures its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. They analyze conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of proxy conditions under which an agent with an η-catastrophic value function would be deployed.

Result: The authors identify conditions under which an agent with a value function that is guaranteed to push expected human value below η in the limit of optimization power would be deployed. This demonstrates the danger of overoptimization.

Conclusion: Overoptimization for a proxy can lead to catastrophic misalignment. Therefore, AI designs that limit optimization pressure (e.g., quantilizers) are preferable to relying solely on pre-deployment training.
